```python
    ck['C02'] = {'name': '柱面通量正比于 r', 'measured': rel, 'tol': 1e-8, 'passed': bool(rel < 1e-8),
                 'note': '线性场下界面通量密度恒定，面积权 A_face=eta_{i+1/2} 使总流量正比于 r'}

    # C03 两个独立分布场
    y = U.initial_state(N)
    dy = rhs_coupled_q1(50.0, y)
    ck['C03'] = {'name': '双场独立', 'len_y': int(len(y)), 'expect_len': 2 * N,
                 'dT_absmax': float(np.abs(dy[:N]).max()), 'dC_absmax': float(np.abs(dy[N:]).max()),
                 'slices_alias': bool(np.shares_memory(y[:N], y[N:])),
                 'passed': bool(len(y) == 2 * N and np.abs(dy[:N]).max() > 0
                                and np.abs(dy[N:]).max() > 0 and not np.shares_memory(y[:N], y[N:]))}

    # C05 Robin 闭合恒等式（温度侧与水分侧同构）
    T, C = y[:N] + 3.0, y[N:] * 0.9
    Ts, Cs2, Ta, Ca, lamN, DN = U.surface_pair(CFG, T, C, 300.0)
    resC = abs(CFG['h_m'] * (Cs2 - Ca) - 2 * DN / (CFG['R_m'] * deta) * (C[-1] - Cs2))
    resT = abs(CFG['h'] * (Ts - Ta) - 2 * lamN / (CFG['R_m'] * deta) * (T[-1] - Ts))
    relC = resC / max(CFG['h_m'] * abs(Cs2 - Ca), 1e-30)
    relT = resT / max(CFG['h'] * abs(Ts - Ta), 1e-30)
    ck['C05'] = {'name': 'Robin 闭合一致', 'measured_C': float(relC), 'measured_T': float(relT),
                 'tol': 1e-6, 'passed': bool(relC < 1e-6 and relT < 1e-6)}

    # C10 单向解耦：扰动 C 不得改变温度场右端
    worst = 0.0
    for scale in (0.9, 1.1):
        y2 = y.copy()
        y2[N:] = y[N:] * scale
        worst = max(worst, float(np.abs(rhs_coupled_q1(50.0, y2)[:N] - dy[:N]).max()))
    ck['C10'] = {'name': '问题一单向解耦', 'measured': worst, 'tol': 1e-14, 'passed': bool(worst < 1e-14)}

    # C11 附录绑定
    ck['C11'] = {'name': '附录绑定', 'props_appendix': PROPS_APPENDIX,
                 'passed': PROPS_APPENDIX == 'appendix_2'}

    # C14 低含水率下界保护
    tiny = np.full(N, 1e-18)
    out = U.eval_props(CFG, tiny, np.full(N, P.T0_C))
    ck['C14'] = {'name': '低含水率下界保护', 'C_floor': P.C_FLOOR,
                 'D_at_1e-18': float(out[3][0]), 'finite': bool(np.all(np.isfinite(np.concatenate(out)))),
                 'passed': bool(np.all(np.isfinite(np.concatenate(out))))}
    return ck


# ------------------------------------------------------------ 主求解
def solve(N=P.N_CELLS, t_eval=None, t_end=P.Q1_T_END):
    cfg = CFG if N == P.N_CELLS else U.make_config(PROPS_APPENDIX, N=N)
    rhs = rhs_coupled_q1 if N == P.N_CELLS else (lambda t, y: U.fixed_domain_rhs(t, y, cfg))
    sol = U.integrate(rhs, (0.0, float(t_end)), U.initial_state(N),
                      t_eval=t_eval, N=N, max_step=P.MAX_STEP_SHORT)
    assert sol.success, sol.message
    return sol, cfg


def fields_on_output_grid(sol, cfg, times):
    """把格心解映射到 21 个固定距离列：中心二阶外推 + 线性插值 + 表面 M3 重构。"""
    N = cfg['N']
    Tg = np.empty((len(times), len(P.OUT_R_CM)))
    Cg = np.empty_like(Tg)
    surf = np.empty((len(times), 2))
    for k, t in enumerate(times):
        y = sol.y[:, k]
        T, C = y[:N], y[N:]
        Ts, Cs, _, _, _, _ = U.surface_pair(cfg, T, C, float(t))
        Tg[k] = U.to_output_radii(T, Ts, cfg['eta_c'], P.OUT_R_CM, P.R0_CM)
        Cg[k] = U.to_output_radii(C, Cs, cfg['eta_c'], P.OUT_R_CM, P.R0_CM)
        surf[k] = (Ts, Cs)
    return Tg, Cg, surf


def run():
    res = {'problem': 1, 'props_appendix': PROPS_APPENDIX,
           'characteristics': {'alpha_m2_s': ALPHA, 'Bi_T': BI_T, 'Bi_m': BI_M,
                               'R0_sq_over_alpha_s': P.R0_M ** 2 / ALPHA,
                               'D_at_C0_m2_s': P.D2_PREFACTOR * np.exp(-P.D2_MOIST_EXP / P.C0),
                               'Fo_T_1800s': ALPHA * 1800.0 / P.R0_M ** 2,
                               'penetration_depth_cm': float(np.sqrt(
                                   P.D2_PREFACTOR * np.exp(-P.D2_MOIST_EXP / P.C0) * 1800.0) * P.CM_PER_M)}}
    ck = unit_tests()

    times = P.Q1_OUT_TIMES
    sol, cfg = solve(t_eval=times)
    Tg, Cg, surf = fields_on_output_grid(sol, cfg, times)
    res['fields'] = {'n_times': len(times), 'n_radii': len(P.OUT_R_CM)}

    # ---- C09 半解析对照（7 时刻 x 21 位置 = 147 点），本题唯一严格外部对照
    T_ana, beta, A_n, tau = U.semi_analytic_temperature(
        P.Q1_TABLE_TIMES, P.OUT_R_CM, ALPHA, BI_T, P.T0_C, U._T_GRID, U._TA_OBS)
    idx = [times.index(t) for t in P.Q1_TABLE_TIMES]
    gap = np.abs(Tg[idx] - T_ana)
    ck['C09'] = {'name': '半解析解对照 147 点', 'measured_max_abs_C': float(gap.max()),
                 'tol': 1e-3, 'n_points': int(gap.size), 'n_modes': P.N_MODES,
                 'passed': bool(gap.max() < 1e-3 and gap.size == 147)}
    res['semi_analytic'] = {'beta_first6': [float(b) for b in beta[:6]],
                            'A_first6': [float(a) for a in A_n[:6]],
                            'tau_first3_s': [float(x) for x in tau[:3]],
                            'max_abs_dev_C': float(gap.max()),
                            'mean_abs_dev_C': float(gap.mean()),
                            'per_time_max_dev_C': [float(g) for g in gap.max(axis=1)],
                            'T_analytic_table': np.round(T_ana[:, [0, 5, 10, 15, 20]], 6).tolist(),
                            # 残差诊断四联图要按半径、按时间铺开，故给全 7x21=147 点而非 5 列摘要
                            'times_s': list(P.Q1_TABLE_TIMES), 'r_cm': list(P.OUT_R_CM),
                            'T_analytic_full': np.round(T_ana, 9).tolist(),
                            'T_numeric_full': np.round(Tg[idx], 9).tolist(),
                            'residual_C': np.round(Tg[idx] - T_ana, 12).tolist(),
                            'n_points': int(gap.size)}

    # ---- V3.2 网格收敛阶梯（五档）+ C04 表面值网格无关性
    ladder = []
    prev_center = prev_surfC = None
    for n in P.MESH_LADDER:
        s_n, c_n = solve(N=n, t_eval=P.Q1_TABLE_TIMES)
        Tn, Cn, sf = fields_on_output_grid(s_n, c_n, P.Q1_TABLE_TIMES)
        rec = {'N': n, 'T_center_1800s': float(Tn[-1, 0]), 'C_surface_1800s': float(Cn[-1, -1]),
               'T_surface_1800s': float(Tn[-1, -1]), 'nfev': int(s_n.nfev)}
        if prev_center is not None:
            rec['dT_center_vs_prev'] = abs(rec['T_center_1800s'] - prev_center)
            rec['dC_surface_vs_prev'] = abs(rec['C_surface_1800s'] - prev_surfC)
        prev_center, prev_surfC = rec['T_center_1800s'], rec['C_surface_1800s']
        ladder.append(rec)
    dT_seq = [r['dT_center_vs_prev'] for r in ladder if 'dT_center_vs_prev' in r]
    dC_seq = [r['dC_surface_vs_prev'] for r in ladder if 'dC_surface_vs_prev' in r]
    ck['V3.2'] = {'name': '网格收敛阶梯（五档）', 'dT_center_seq': dT_seq,
                  'finest_pair_dT': dT_seq[-1], 'tol': 1e-4,
                  'monotone_decreasing': bool(all(dT_seq[i] > dT_seq[i + 1] for i in range(len(dT_seq) - 1))),
                  'passed': bool(dT_seq[-1] < 1e-4 and all(dT_seq[i] > dT_seq[i + 1] for i in range(len(dT_seq) - 1)))}
    ck['C04'] = {'name': '表面值网格无关性', 'measured': dC_seq[-1], 'tol': 1e-3,
                 'dC_surface_seq': dC_seq, 'passed': bool(dC_seq[-1] < 1e-3)}
    res['mesh_ladder'] = ladder

    # ---- 第 2 层：物理健康性（C06/C07/C13 + V2.4 极大值原理）
    N = cfg['N']
    Tc, Cc = sol.y[:N, :], sol.y[N:, :]
    ck['C06'] = {'name': '剖面单调（中心最湿/最冷）',
                 'C_worst_increase': float(np.max(np.diff(Cc, axis=0))),
                 'T_worst_decrease': float(np.max(-np.diff(Tc, axis=0))), 'tol': 1e-10,
                 'passed': bool(np.max(np.diff(Cc, axis=0)) < 1e-10 and np.max(-np.diff(Tc, axis=0)) < 1e-10)}
    argmax_center = bool(np.all(np.argmax(Cc, axis=0) == 0))
    ck['C07'] = {'name': '全域最大含水率在中心', 'all_at_center': argmax_center,
                 'n_times_checked': int(Cc.shape[1]), 'passed': argmax_center}
    cmax = Cc.max(axis=0)
    vol_avg = (Cc * cfg['V_cell'][:, None]).sum(axis=0) / cfg['V_cell'].sum()
    ck['C13'] = {'name': '干燥方向性', 'max_rise_of_Cmax': float(np.max(np.diff(cmax))),
                 'max_rise_of_volavg': float(np.max(np.diff(vol_avg))), 'tol': 1e-12,
                 'passed': bool(np.max(np.diff(cmax)) < 1e-12 and np.max(np.diff(vol_avg)) < 1e-12)}
    ta_all = np.array([U.driver_Ta(t) for t in times])
    lo, hi = min(P.T0_C, ta_all.min()), max(P.T0_C, ta_all.max())
    ck['V2.4'] = {'name': '温度落在驱动包络内', 'T_min': float(Tg.min()), 'T_max': float(Tg.max()),
                  'envelope': [float(lo), float(hi)],
                  'passed': bool(Tg.min() >= lo - 1e-9 and Tg.max() <= hi + 1e-9)}

    # ---- 时间离散误差分层（§4.4）：容差再收紧一档，基准解在报告时刻的变化必须 < 1e-4
    import scipy.integrate as si

    def _at_tol(rt, at):
        return si.solve_ivp(rhs_coupled_q1, (0.0, float(P.Q1_T_END)), U.initial_state(N), method='BDF',
                            rtol=rt, atol=at, jac_sparsity=U.jacobian_sparsity(N),
                            t_eval=P.Q1_TABLE_TIMES, max_step=P.MAX_STEP_SHORT)

    d_tight = float(np.abs(_at_tol(1e-10, 1e-12).y - sol.y[:, idx]).max())
    d_loose = float(np.abs(_at_tol(1e-6, 1e-8).y - sol.y[:, idx]).max())
    ck['time_tol_layer'] = {'name': '时间离散误差分层（收紧一档）', 'measured_tighten_1e-10': d_tight,
                            'tol': 1e-4, 'passed': bool(d_tight < 1e-4),
                            'diagnostic_loosen_1e-6': d_loose,
                            'note': '§4.4 判据为收紧容差后变化 <1e-4；放松到 1e-6 的偏差 %.3e °C 一并记录，'
                                    'V3.3 的 t_end<60 s 判据在问题三、四（有事件终止）处考核' % d_loose}

    # ---- 写盘（C16 由 write_result_xlsx 内部读回核验）
    rt = U.write_result_xlsx(P.RESULT_FILES[1], {'温度': Tg, '水分浓度': Cg}, times, P.OUT_R_CM)
    ck['C16'] = {'name': '写盘-读回往返一致', 'measured': max(v['roundtrip_max_abs_diff'] for v in rt.values()),
                 'tol': 0.0, 'per_sheet': rt, 'passed': True}

    # ---- 论文表 1、表 2
    cols = [P.OUT_R_CM.index(r) for r in P.TABLE_R_CM]
    res['table_1_temperature'] = {'times_s': P.Q1_TABLE_TIMES, 'radii_cm': P.TABLE_R_CM,
                                  'values': np.round(Tg[np.ix_(idx, cols)], 4).tolist()}
    res['table_2_moisture'] = {'times_s': P.Q1_TABLE_TIMES, 'radii_cm': P.TABLE_R_CM,
                               'values': np.round(Cg[np.ix_(idx, cols)], 4).tolist()}
    res['summary'] = {'T_center_1800s': float(Tg[-1, 0]), 'T_surface_1800s': float(Tg[-1, -1]),
                      'T_gap_center_surface_1800s': float(Tg[-1, -1] - Tg[-1, 0]),
                      'C_center_1800s': float(Cg[-1, 0]), 'C_surface_1800s': float(Cg[-1, -1]),
                      'C_center_deviation_from_C0': float(P.C0 - Cg[-1, 0]),
                      'herb_T_max': float(Tg.max()), 'herb_T_min': float(Tg.min()),
                      'herb_C_max': float(Cg.max()), 'herb_C_min': float(Cg.min()),
                      'volavg_C_1800s': float(vol_avg[-1])}
    res['profiles'] = {'times_s': [100, 300, 600, 900, 1200, 1500, 1800], 'r_cm': P.OUT_R_CM,
                       'T': np.round(Tg[idx], 6).tolist(), 'C': np.round(Cg[idx], 6).tolist()}
    res['timeseries'] = {'t_s': times[::10], 'T_center': np.round(Tg[::10, 0], 6).tolist(),
                         'T_surface': np.round(Tg[::10, -1], 6).tolist(),
                         'C_center': np.round(Cg[::10, 0], 6).tolist(),
                         'C_surface': np.round(Cg[::10, -1], 6).tolist(),
                         'Ta': np.round(ta_all[::10], 6).tolist(),
                         'Ca': np.round([U.driver_Ca(t) for t in times[::10]], 8).tolist()}
    res['heatmap'] = {'t_s': times[::20], 'r_cm': P.OUT_R_CM,
                      'T': np.round(Tg[::20], 4).tolist(), 'C': np.round(Cg[::20], 4).tolist()}

    # ---- 空气侧输入：附件 1 在 [0,1800] s 内的实测点 + 本文所用的分段线性插值
    m_obs = U._T_GRID <= float(P.Q1_T_END)
    res['air_input'] = {'t_obs_s': U._T_GRID[m_obs].tolist(),
                        'Ta_obs_C': U._TA_OBS[m_obs].tolist(),
                        'Ca_obs': U._CA_OBS[m_obs].tolist(),
                        't_interp_s': times[::10],
                        'Ta_interp_C': np.round(ta_all[::10], 6).tolist(),
                        'Ca_interp': np.round([U.driver_Ca(t) for t in times[::10]], 8).tolist(),
                        'Ta_range_C': [float(ta_all.min()), float(ta_all.max())],
                        'n_obs_points': int(m_obs.sum()),
                        'note': '问题一时段内热风温度由 28 °C 近线性升至 %.4f °C；'
                                '空气含湿量同步上行' % float(ta_all.max())}

    # ---- 三维温度场（t, r, T）：粗网格供曲面图使用，避免 1800x21 过密
    st3 = 30
    res['temp_field_3d'] = {'t_s': times[st3 - 1::st3], 'r_cm': P.OUT_R_CM,
                            'T': np.round(Tg[st3 - 1::st3], 4).tolist(),
                            'C': np.round(Cg[st3 - 1::st3], 4).tolist(),
                            'T_span_C': [float(Tg.min()), float(Tg.max())],
                            'C_span': [float(Cg.min()), float(Cg.max())]}

    # ---- 水分边界层：失水只发生在表面附近约 sqrt(D t) 的薄层内，故用格心原始网格呈现
    r_cell = cfg['eta_c'] * P.R0_CM
    pen_cm = float(np.sqrt(P.D2_PREFACTOR * np.exp(-P.D2_MOIST_EXP / P.C0) * P.Q1_T_END)
                   * P.CM_PER_M)
    bl_idx = [times.index(t) for t in P.Q1_TABLE_TIMES]
    depth = P.R0_CM - r_cell
    dev = P.C0 - Cc[:, bl_idx]                     # 相对初值的失水量（格心值，未插值）
    thick = []
    for j in range(dev.shape[1]):
        over = np.nonzero(dev[:, j] > 1e-4)[0]
        thick.append(float(depth[over.min()]) if over.size else 0.0)
    res['moisture_bl'] = {'times_s': P.Q1_TABLE_TIMES, 'depth_from_surface_cm': depth.tolist(),
                          'C_cells': np.round(Cc[:, bl_idx].T, 6).tolist(),
                          'deficit_from_C0': np.round(dev.T, 8).tolist(),
                          'C_surface_recon': np.round(surf[bl_idx, 1], 6).tolist(),
                          'C_center': np.round(Cc[0, bl_idx], 6).tolist(),
                          'penetration_depth_cm': pen_cm,
                          'bl_thickness_cm': thick,
                          'deficit_tol': 1e-4, 'C0': P.C0, 'R0_cm': P.R0_CM,
                          'note': '失水层厚度 %.4f cm（1800 s，判据 C0-C>1e-4），与尺度估计 '
                                  'sqrt(D t)=%.4f cm 同量级；中心含水率仍为 %.4f，'
                                  '故问题一必须解 PDE 而不能用集中参数'
                                  % (thick[-1], pen_cm, float(Cc[0, bl_idx[-1]]))}
    res['xlsx_report'] = rt
    res['checks'] = ck
    res['all_checks_passed'] = bool(all(v.get('passed') for v in ck.values()))
    return res, ck, sol, cfg, times, Tg, Cg, surf


if __name__ == '__main__':
    out = run()[0]
    P.FIG_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    (P.FIG_DIR / 'problem_1_results.json').write_text(
        json.dumps(out, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding='utf-8')
    for k, v in out['checks'].items():
        print(f"{k}: {'PASS' if v.get('passed') else 'FAIL'}  {v.get('name', '')}")
    print('Q1 checks passed:', out['all_checks_passed'])
    print('T center/surface @1800s: %.4f / %.4f' % (out['summary']['T_center_1800s'],
                                                    out['summary']['T_surface_1800s']))
    print('C center/surface @1800s: %.4f / %.4f' % (out['summary']['C_center_1800s'],
                                                    out['summary']['C_surface_1800s']))
    print('semi-analytic max dev: %.3e C' % out['semi_analytic']['max_abs_dev_C'])
```

**B.5　problem2.py**

```python
# code/problem2.py — 问题二：全过程变物性双向强耦合（附录 3）+ 四级模型阶梯
import json

import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))   # 本机为嵌入式发行版，不自动加脚本目录

import params as P
import utils as U

PROPS_APPENDIX = 'appendix_3'
CFG = U.make_config(PROPS_APPENDIX)


def rhs_coupled_q2(t, y):
    """问题二右端：附录 3 变物性，D 含 Arrhenius(273.15 + T) 故温度反馈进水分场。"""
    return U.fixed_domain_rhs(t, y, CFG)


# ------------------------------------------------------------ 四级模型阶梯（MC8）
def model_L0_lumped(t_end, n_out=601):
    """L0 集中参数薄层模型：Bi=O(1) 时的错误极限，两个 ODE 无空间分布。"""
    def rhs(t, s):
        T, C = s
        rho, cp, lam, D = U.props_appendix_3(np.array([C]), np.array([T]))
        a_v = 2.0 / P.R0_M                                  # 侧面积/体积（长圆柱）
        dC = -a_v * P.HM_MASS * (C - U.driver_Ca(t))
        dT = a_v * P.H_CONV * (U.driver_Ta(t) - T) / (rho[0] * cp[0])
        return [float(dT), float(dC)]

    ts = np.linspace(0.0, float(t_end), n_out)
    sol = U.integrate(rhs, (0.0, float(t_end)), np.array([P.T0_C, P.C0]), t_eval=ts, N=1,
                      max_step=P.MAX_STEP_SHORT)
    assert sol.success, sol.message
    return sol, ts


def model_L1_const_prop(t_end, t_eval):
    """L1 常物性：物性冻结在初值 (C0, T0)，方程线性化。"""
    cfg = U.make_config(PROPS_APPENDIX, freeze_props=True)
    sol = U.integrate(lambda t, y: U.fixed_domain_rhs(t, y, cfg), (0.0, float(t_end)),
                      U.initial_state(cfg['N']), t_eval=t_eval, N=cfg['N'], max_step=P.MAX_STEP_SHORT)
    return sol, cfg


def model_L2_oneway(t_end, t_eval):
    """L2 单向耦合：rho/cp/lam 随场变化，但 D 的温度取初温（切断 T -> C 反馈）。"""
    cfg = U.make_config(PROPS_APPENDIX, d_temp_frozen=True)
    sol = U.integrate(lambda t, y: U.fixed_domain_rhs(t, y, cfg), (0.0, float(t_end)),
                      U.initial_state(cfg['N']), t_eval=t_eval, N=cfg['N'], max_step=P.MAX_STEP_SHORT)
    return sol, cfg


def model_L3_full(t_end, t_eval):
    """L3 全耦合：本问基准模型。"""
    sol = U.integrate(rhs_coupled_q2, (0.0, float(t_end)), U.initial_state(CFG['N']),
                      t_eval=t_eval, N=CFG['N'], max_step=P.MAX_STEP_SHORT)
    return sol, CFG


# ------------------------------------------------------------ 第 1 层单元测试
def unit_tests():
    ck = {}
    N = CFG['N']

    # C11 附录绑定
    ck['C11'] = {'name': '附录绑定', 'props_appendix': PROPS_APPENDIX,
                 'passed': PROPS_APPENDIX == 'appendix_3'}

    # C12 五个偏导探针：rho,cp,lam 对 C，D 对 C 与对 T 都必须非零
    C = np.full(N, P.C0)
    T = np.full(N, P.T0_C)
    base = U.props_appendix_3(C, T)
    dC = U.props_appendix_3(C * 1.05, T)
    dT = U.props_appendix_3(C, T + 5.0)
    partials = {'d_rho_dC': float(np.abs(dC[0] - base[0]).max()),
                'd_cp_dC': float(np.abs(dC[1] - base[1]).max()),
                'd_lam_dC': float(np.abs(dC[2] - base[2]).max()),
                'd_D_dC': float(np.abs(dC[3] - base[3]).max()),
                'd_D_dT': float(np.abs(dT[3] - base[3]).max())}
    inert = {'d_rho_dT': float(np.abs(dT[0] - base[0]).max()),
             'd_cp_dT': float(np.abs(dT[1] - base[1]).max()),
             'd_lam_dT': float(np.abs(dT[2] - base[2]).max())}
    ck['C12'] = {'name': '五个物性偏导非零', 'partials': partials, 'tol': 1e-12,
                 'inert_partials_expected_zero': inert,
                 'passed': bool(all(v > 1e-12 for v in partials.values())
                                and all(v == 0.0 for v in inert.values()))}

    # C08 驱动复现与衔接连续
    err_T = max(abs(U.driver_Ta(float(t)) - v) / max(abs(v), 1e-30)
                for t, v in zip(U._T_GRID, U._TA_OBS))
    err_C = max(abs(U.driver_Ca(float(t)) - v) / max(abs(v), 1e-30)
                for t, v in zip(U._T_GRID, U._CA_OBS))
    jump_T = abs(U.driver_Ta(U.T_OBS + 1e-9) - U.driver_Ta(U.T_OBS - 1e-9))
    jump_C = abs(U.driver_Ca(U.T_OBS + 1e-9) - U.driver_Ca(U.T_OBS - 1e-9))
    ck['C08'] = {'name': '附件 1 复现与衔接连续', 'sample_rel_err_Ta': err_T,
                 'sample_rel_err_Ca': err_C, 'tol_sample': 1e-9,
                 'join_jump_Ta_C': jump_T, 'join_jump_Ca': jump_C, 'tol_join': 1e-6,
                 'passed': bool(err_T < 1e-9 and err_C < 1e-9 and jump_T < 1e-6 and jump_C < 1e-6)}

    # C14 低含水率保护：附录 3 的 D 在 C->0 时须有限
    tiny = np.full(N, 1e-18)
    out = U.props_appendix_3(tiny, np.full(N, 50.0))
    ck['C14'] = {'name': '低含水率下界保护', 'C_floor': P.C_FLOOR, 'D_at_1e-18': float(out[3][0]),
                 'passed': bool(np.all(np.isfinite(np.concatenate(out))))}

    # 开尔文代入核验：D 之比必须等于 exp(-3850/T2K + 3850/T1K)
    T1, T2 = 28.0, 50.0
    d1 = U.props_appendix_3(np.array([P.C0]), np.array([T1]))[3][0]
    d2 = U.props_appendix_3(np.array([P.C0]), np.array([T2]))[3][0]
    want = np.exp(-P.ACT_TEMP_K / (T2 + P.KELVIN_OFFSET) + P.ACT_TEMP_K / (T1 + P.KELVIN_OFFSET))
    rel = abs(d2 / d1 - want) / want
    ck['C_kelvin'] = {'name': 'Arrhenius 用开尔文', 'measured_rel': float(rel), 'tol': 1e-12,
                      'D_ratio_50_over_28': float(d2 / d1), 'passed': bool(rel < 1e-12)}
    return ck


def solve(N=P.N_CELLS, t_eval=None, t_end=P.Q2_T_END):
    cfg = CFG if N == P.N_CELLS else U.make_config(PROPS_APPENDIX, N=N)
    rhs = rhs_coupled_q2 if N == P.N_CELLS else (lambda t, y: U.fixed_domain_rhs(t, y, cfg))
    sol = U.integrate(rhs, (0.0, float(t_end)), U.initial_state(N), t_eval=t_eval, N=N,
                      max_step=P.MAX_STEP_SHORT)
    assert sol.success, sol.message
    return sol, cfg


# ------------------------------------------------------------ 主求解
def run():
    res = {'problem': 2, 'props_appendix': PROPS_APPENDIX}
    ck = unit_tests()
    times = P.Q2_OUT_TIMES
    idx = [times.index(t) for t in P.Q2_TABLE_TIMES]

    sol, cfg = solve(t_eval=times)
    N = cfg['N']
    Tg, Cg, surf = U.fields_on_grid(sol, cfg, times)
    Tc, Cc = sol.y[:N, :], sol.y[N:, :]
    vol_avg = U.vol_average(Cc, cfg['V_cell'])

    # 平台分支未被触发（t_end=10800 s < T_OBS=14400 s，全程为附件 1 实测区内）
    ck['C_no_plateau'] = {'name': '问题二未进入外推段', 't_end_s': P.Q2_T_END, 'T_obs_s': U.T_OBS,
                          'passed': bool(P.Q2_T_END <= U.T_OBS)}

    # ---- 第 2 层：物理健康性
    # 温度侧只在驱动非降的时刻考核：附件 1 平台段 Ta 有 95 次负增量（最小 -0.45 °C），
    # 热风回落时表面先冷于内部，属驱动的真实特征而非离散误差。
    t_first_dip = float(U._T_GRID[1:][np.diff(U._TA_OBS) < 0][0])
    rising = np.asarray(times, float) <= t_first_dip
    step_down = float(abs(np.diff(U._TA_OBS).min()))
    T_inv_rise = float(np.max(-np.diff(Tc[:, rising], axis=0)))
    T_inv_post = float(np.max(-np.diff(Tc[:, ~rising], axis=0)))
    ck['C06'] = {'name': '剖面单调（中心最湿；纯升温段中心最冷）',
                 'C_worst_increase': float(np.max(np.diff(Cc, axis=0))), 'tol_C': 1e-10,
                 'T_inversion_rising_window': T_inv_rise, 'tol_T_rising': 1e-10,
                 't_first_driver_dip_s': t_first_dip, 'n_times_rising': int(rising.sum()),
                 'T_inversion_after_first_dip': T_inv_post,
                 'driver_worst_down_step_C': step_down,
                 'inversion_over_driver_step': T_inv_post / step_down, 'tol_ratio': 0.05,
                 'driver_negative_increments': int((np.diff(U._TA_OBS) < 0).sum()),
                 'note': '附件 1 平台段 Ta 有 95 次负增量（最大回落 0.45 °C）；热风回落时表面先冷于内部，'
                         '该反转幅度仅为驱动回落量的 0.17%，是驱动的真实特征而非离散误差',
                 'passed': bool(np.max(np.diff(Cc, axis=0)) < 1e-10 and T_inv_rise < 1e-10
                                and T_inv_post / step_down < 0.05)}
    # 并列判定：中心值必须等于全域最大值（差值只允许浮点舍入量级）
    gap_to_max = float(np.max(Cc.max(axis=0) - Cc[0, :]))
    ck['C07'] = {'name': '全域最大含水率在中心', 'max_minus_center': gap_to_max, 'tol': 1e-12,
                 'n_times_checked': int(Cc.shape[1]),
                 'n_times_strict_argmax_at_center': int((np.argmax(Cc, axis=0) == 0).sum()),
                 'note': '早期中心与邻近若干格心在 1e-16 量级并列，故用 max-center 差值判定',
                 'passed': bool(gap_to_max < 1e-12)}
    cmax = Cc.max(axis=0)
    ck['C13'] = {'name': '干燥方向性', 'max_rise_of_Cmax': float(np.max(np.diff(cmax))),
                 'max_rise_of_volavg': float(np.max(np.diff(vol_avg))), 'tol': 1e-12,
                 'passed': bool(np.max(np.diff(cmax)) < 1e-12 and np.max(np.diff(vol_avg)) < 1e-12)}
    ta_all = np.array([U.driver_Ta(t) for t in times])
    lo, hi = min(P.T0_C, ta_all.min()), max(P.T0_C, ta_all.max())
    ck['V2.4'] = {'name': '温度落在驱动包络内', 'T_min': float(Tg.min()), 'T_max': float(Tg.max()),
                  'envelope': [float(lo), float(hi)],
                  'passed': bool(Tg.min() >= lo - 1e-9 and Tg.max() <= hi + 1e-9)}

    # ---- 网格收敛（四档）+ C04 表面值网格无关性
    ladder = []
    prev_c = prev_s = None
    for n in (30, 60, 120, 240):
        s_n, c_n = solve(N=n, t_eval=P.Q2_TABLE_TIMES)
        Tn, Cn, _ = U.fields_on_grid(s_n, c_n, P.Q2_TABLE_TIMES)
        rec = {'N': n, 'C_center_end': float(Cn[-1, 0]), 'C_surface_end': float(Cn[-1, -1]),
               'T_center_end': float(Tn[-1, 0]), 'nfev': int(s_n.nfev)}
        if prev_c is not None:
            rec['dC_center_vs_prev'] = abs(rec['C_center_end'] - prev_c)
            rec['dC_surface_vs_prev'] = abs(rec['C_surface_end'] - prev_s)
        prev_c, prev_s = rec['C_center_end'], rec['C_surface_end']
        ladder.append(rec)
    dcen = [r['dC_center_vs_prev'] for r in ladder if 'dC_center_vs_prev' in r]
    dsur = [r['dC_surface_vs_prev'] for r in ladder if 'dC_surface_vs_prev' in r]
    ck['V3.2'] = {'name': '网格收敛阶梯（四档）', 'dC_center_seq': dcen, 'finest_pair': dcen[-1],
                  'tol': 1e-3, 'passed': bool(dcen[-1] < 1e-3)}
    ck['C04'] = {'name': '表面值网格无关性', 'measured': dsur[-1], 'tol': 1e-3,
                 'dC_surface_seq': dsur, 'passed': bool(dsur[-1] < 1e-3)}
    res['mesh_ladder'] = ladder

    # ---- V3.6 四级模型阶梯（每级独立真实运行）+ C15 两两可分辨
    lad_t = P.Q2_TABLE_TIMES
    s0, ts0 = model_L0_lumped(P.Q2_T_END)
    s1, c1 = model_L1_const_prop(P.Q2_T_END, lad_t)
    s2, c2 = model_L2_oneway(P.Q2_T_END, lad_t)
    s3, c3 = model_L3_full(P.Q2_T_END, lad_t)
    lump_C = float(np.interp(P.Q2_T_END, ts0, s0.y[1]))
    lump_T = float(np.interp(P.Q2_T_END, ts0, s0.y[0]))
    paths = {}
    for tag, s, c in (('L1_const_prop', s1, c1), ('L2_oneway', s2, c2), ('L3_full', s3, c3)):
        Tp, Cp, _ = U.fields_on_grid(s, c, lad_t)
        paths[tag] = {'C_center_end': float(Cp[-1, 0]), 'C_surface_end': float(Cp[-1, -1]),
                      'T_center_end': float(Tp[-1, 0]),
                      'volavg_C_end': float(U.vol_average(s.y[c['N']:, -1], c['V_cell'])),
                      'C_center_series': np.round(Cp[:, 0], 6).tolist(),
                      'volavg_C_series': [float(x) for x in
                                          U.vol_average(s.y[c['N']:, :], c['V_cell'])]}
    paths['L0_lumped'] = {'C_end': lump_C, 'T_end': lump_T,
                          'note': 'Bi_T, Bi_m 均为 O(1)，集中参数假设不成立，此路径仅作错误极限对照',
                          'C_series': np.round(np.interp(lad_t, ts0, s0.y[1]), 6).tolist(),
                          'T_series': np.round(np.interp(lad_t, ts0, s0.y[0]), 6).tolist()}
    keys = ['L0_lumped', 'L1_const_prop', 'L2_oneway', 'L3_full']
    vals = [lump_C] + [paths[k]['volavg_C_end'] for k in keys[1:]]
    gaps = [abs(vals[i] - vals[j]) for i in range(4) for j in range(i + 1, 4)]
    ck['V3.6'] = {'name': '四级模型阶梯真实独立运行', 'volavg_C_end_by_path':
                  {k: v for k, v in zip(keys, vals)}, 'min_pairwise_gap': float(min(gaps)),
                  'tol': 1e-6, 'passed': bool(min(gaps) > 1e-6)}
    ck['C15'] = {'name': '模型对比两两可分辨', 'measured_min_gap': float(min(gaps)), 'tol': 1e-6,
                 'n_pairs': len(gaps), 'passed': bool(min(gaps) > 1e-6)}
    res['model_ladder'] = {'times_s': lad_t, 'paths': paths,
                           'baseline_volavg_C_end': paths['L3_full']['volavg_C_end']}

    # ---- 写盘 result2.xlsx（C16 内部读回核验）
    rt = U.write_result_xlsx(P.RESULT_FILES[2], {'温度': Tg, '水分浓度': Cg}, times, P.OUT_R_CM)
    ck['C16'] = {'name': '写盘-读回往返一致',
                 'measured': max(v['roundtrip_max_abs_diff'] for v in rt.values()),
                 'tol': 0.0, 'per_sheet': rt, 'passed': True}

    # ---- 论文表 3、表 4
    cols = [P.OUT_R_CM.index(r) for r in P.TABLE_R_CM]
    res['table_3_temperature'] = {'times_s': P.Q2_TABLE_TIMES, 'radii_cm': P.TABLE_R_CM,
                                  'values': np.round(Tg[np.ix_(idx, cols)], 4).tolist()}
    res['table_4_moisture'] = {'times_s': P.Q2_TABLE_TIMES, 'radii_cm': P.TABLE_R_CM,
                               'values': np.round(Cg[np.ix_(idx, cols)], 4).tolist()}
    res['summary'] = {'T_center_end': float(Tg[-1, 0]), 'T_surface_end': float(Tg[-1, -1]),
                      'C_center_end': float(Cg[-1, 0]), 'C_surface_end': float(Cg[-1, -1]),
                      'volavg_C_end': float(vol_avg[-1]),
                      'C_center_at_1800s': float(Cg[times.index(1800), 0]),
                      'C_surface_at_1800s': float(Cg[times.index(1800), -1]),
                      'T_center_at_1800s': float(Tg[times.index(1800), 0]),
                      'herb_T_max': float(Tg.max()), 'herb_T_min': float(Tg.min()),
                      'herb_C_max': float(Cg.max()), 'herb_C_min': float(Cg.min()),
                      'D_at_center_end_m2_s': float(U.props_appendix_3(
                          np.array([Cg[-1, 0]]), np.array([Tg[-1, 0]]))[3][0]),
                      'D_at_t0_m2_s': float(U.props_appendix_3(
                          np.array([P.C0]), np.array([P.T0_C]))[3][0])}
    res['profiles'] = {'times_s': P.Q2_TABLE_TIMES, 'r_cm': P.OUT_R_CM,
                       'T': np.round(Tg[idx], 6).tolist(), 'C': np.round(Cg[idx], 6).tolist()}

    # ---- D(C,T) 响应面：双向耦合的机理来源（D 同时依含水率与温度）
    Cs_ax = np.linspace(P.C_THR, P.C0, 25)
    Ts_ax = np.linspace(P.T0_C, float(ta_all.max()), 25)
    Dgrid = np.array([[U.props_appendix_3(np.array([c]), np.array([tt]))[3][0]
                       for c in Cs_ax] for tt in Ts_ax])
    traj = np.array([[float(Cc[:, k].mean()), float(Tc[:, k].mean())]
                     for k in range(0, len(times), 60)])
    res['D_response_surface'] = {'C_axis': Cs_ax.tolist(), 'T_axis_C': Ts_ax.tolist(),
                                 'D_m2_s': Dgrid.tolist(),
                                 'D_min': float(Dgrid.min()), 'D_max': float(Dgrid.max()),
                                 'ratio_max_over_min': float(Dgrid.max() / Dgrid.min()),
                                 'trajectory_C_T': traj.tolist(),
                                 'trajectory_t_s': list(times[::60]),
                                 'note': 'D 随温度升高而增大、随含水率下降而减小，两者在烘干过程中反向抵消'}
    step = 30
    res['timeseries'] = {'t_s': times[::step], 'T_center': np.round(Tg[::step, 0], 6).tolist(),
                         'T_surface': np.round(Tg[::step, -1], 6).tolist(),
                         'C_center': np.round(Cg[::step, 0], 6).tolist(),
                         'C_surface': np.round(Cg[::step, -1], 6).tolist(),
                         'C_volavg': np.round(vol_avg[::step], 6).tolist(),
                         'Ta': np.round(ta_all[::step], 6).tolist(),
                         'Ca': np.round([U.driver_Ca(t) for t in times[::step]], 8).tolist(),
                         'T_surf_recon': np.round(surf[::step, 0], 6).tolist(),
                         'C_surf_recon': np.round(surf[::step, 1], 8).tolist(),
                         'q_conv_W_m2': np.round(P.H_CONV * (ta_all[::step] - surf[::step, 0]),
                                                 6).tolist(),
                         'j_mass_kg_m2_s': [float(P.HM_MASS * (cs - U.driver_Ca(t)))
                                            for t, cs in zip(times[::step], surf[::step, 1])]}
    res['heatmap'] = {'t_s': times[::120], 'r_cm': P.OUT_R_CM,
                      'T': np.round(Tg[::120], 4).tolist(), 'C': np.round(Cg[::120], 4).tolist()}
    res['xlsx_report'] = rt
    res['checks'] = ck
    res['all_checks_passed'] = bool(all(v.get('passed') for v in ck.values()))
    return res, ck, sol, cfg, times, Tg, Cg, surf


if __name__ == '__main__':
    out = run()[0]
    P.FIG_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    (P.FIG_DIR / 'problem_2_results.json').write_text(
        json.dumps(out, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding='utf-8')
    for k, v in out['checks'].items():
        print(f"{k}: {'PASS' if v.get('passed') else 'FAIL'}  {v.get('name', '')}")
    print('Q2 checks passed:', out['all_checks_passed'])
    s = out['summary']
    print('T center/surface @10800s: %.4f / %.4f' % (s['T_center_end'], s['T_surface_end']))
    print('C center/surface @10800s: %.4f / %.4f' % (s['C_center_end'], s['C_surface_end']))
    print('volavg C @10800s: %.4f' % s['volavg_C_end'])
    print('D(t=0) -> D(center,end): %.3e -> %.3e m2/s' % (s['D_at_t0_m2_s'],
                                                          s['D_at_center_end_m2_s']))
```

**B.6　problem3.py**

```python
# code/problem3.py — 问题三：全域最大值判据下的烘干时长（附录 3，事件检测定位首次下穿）
import json

import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))   # 本机为嵌入式发行版，不自动加脚本目录

import params as P
import problem2 as Q2
import utils as U

PROPS_APPENDIX = 'appendix_3'
CFG = Q2.CFG                      # 与问题二严格同一配置（XA2 要求逐格恒等）


def rhs_coupled_q3(t, y):
    """问题三右端：与问题二同一求解器，仅在终止方式上不同（事件检测代替定时）。"""
    return Q2.rhs_coupled_q2(t, y)


# ------------------------------------------------------------ 三种判据的事件函数
def event_max_criterion(t, y):
    """题面判据：全域最大含水率首次下穿阈值（『各处』= 对全部空间点同时成立）。"""
    return U.event_moisture_threshold(t, y)


def event_volavg_criterion(t, y, V=CFG['V_cell'], C_thr=P.C_THR):
    """对照判据：柱面体积权平均含水率下穿阈值（误读『各处』为『整体』的后果）。"""
    N = len(y) // 2
    return float(y[N:] @ V / V.sum()) - C_thr


def event_surface_criterion(t, y, cfg=CFG, C_thr=P.C_THR):
    """对照判据：表面含水率下穿阈值（用 M3 重构的表面值，不是最外格心值）。"""
    N = len(y) // 2
    C_s = U.surface_pair(cfg, y[:N], y[N:], t)[1]
    return float(C_s) - C_thr


for _f in (event_max_criterion, event_volavg_criterion, event_surface_criterion):
    _f.terminal = True
    _f.direction = -1


def solve_to_event(event, N=P.N_CELLS, dense=False, rtol=None, atol=None, cfg=None):
    """积分到事件触发；t_span 上限为 §13-⓪ 的保护上限 T_INTEGRATION_CAP。"""
    c = cfg if cfg is not None else (CFG if N == P.N_CELLS else U.make_config(PROPS_APPENDIX, N=N))
    rhs = (lambda t, y: U.fixed_domain_rhs(t, y, c))
    if rtol is None:
        sol = U.integrate(rhs, (0.0, P.T_INTEGRATION_CAP), U.initial_state(c['N']), N=c['N'],
                          events=event, max_step=P.MAX_STEP_LONG, dense_output=dense)
    else:
        import scipy.integrate as si
        sol = si.solve_ivp(rhs, (0.0, P.T_INTEGRATION_CAP), U.initial_state(c['N']), method='BDF',
                           rtol=rtol, atol=atol, jac_sparsity=U.jacobian_sparsity(c['N']),
                           events=event, max_step=P.MAX_STEP_LONG, dense_output=dense)
    assert sol.success, sol.message
    assert len(sol.t_events[0]) > 0, '事件未触发：t_end 超出保护上限 %g s' % P.T_INTEGRATION_CAP
    return sol, float(sol.t_events[0][0]), c


# ------------------------------------------------------------ 单元测试与判据核验
def unit_tests(sol, t_end, cfg):
    ck = {}
    N = cfg['N']

    ck['C11'] = {'name': '附录绑定（与问题二同源）', 'props_appendix': PROPS_APPENDIX,
                 'passed': PROPS_APPENDIX == 'appendix_3'}

    # C19 首次下穿：事件时刻的全域最大含水率必须贴阈值，且此前全程严格高于阈值
    y_e = sol.y_events[0][0]
    C_e = y_e[N:]
    hit = abs(float(C_e.max()) - P.C_THR)
    scan_t = np.linspace(0.0, t_end * (1 - 1e-9), 20000)
    Cmax_scan = np.array([sol.sol(t)[N:].max() for t in scan_t])
    below = int((Cmax_scan < P.C_THR).sum())
    ck['C19'] = {'name': '首次下穿（密扫无更早穿越）', 'measured_endpoint_gap': float(hit),
                 'tol': 1e-5, 'n_scan_points': int(scan_t.size), 'n_points_below_thr': below,
                 'min_Cmax_before_t_end': float(Cmax_scan.min()),
                 'passed': bool(hit < 1e-5 and below == 0)}

    # C07 事件时刻最大值必须在中心
    ck['C07'] = {'name': '事件时刻最大值在中心', 'argmax_cell': int(C_e.argmax()),
                 'C_center': float(C_e[0]), 'C_surface_cell': float(C_e[-1]),
                 'passed': bool(C_e.argmax() == 0)}
    return ck


def solve(N=P.N_CELLS, dense=True):
    return solve_to_event(event_max_criterion, N=N, dense=dense)


def run():
    res = {'problem': 3, 'props_appendix': PROPS_APPENDIX}
    sol, t_end, cfg = solve()
    N = cfg['N']
    ck = unit_tests(sol, t_end, cfg)
    res['t_end'] = {'seconds': t_end, 'hours': t_end / P.SEC_PER_HOUR,
                    'days': t_end / (24 * P.SEC_PER_HOUR), 'nfev': int(sol.nfev),
                    'n_solver_steps': int(len(sol.t))}

    # ---- C17 三种判据口径对比（各自独立真实运行）
    crits = {}
    for tag, ev in (('max', event_max_criterion), ('volavg', event_volavg_criterion),
                    ('surface', event_surface_criterion)):
        s_c, t_c, _ = solve_to_event(ev)
        crits[tag] = {'t_end_s': t_c, 't_end_h': t_c / P.SEC_PER_HOUR, 'nfev': int(s_c.nfev)}
    tm, tv, ts = crits['max']['t_end_s'], crits['volavg']['t_end_s'], crits['surface']['t_end_s']
    ck['C17'] = {'name': '判据口径序关系', 't_surface_h': ts / P.SEC_PER_HOUR,
                 't_volavg_h': tv / P.SEC_PER_HOUR, 't_max_h': tm / P.SEC_PER_HOUR,
                 'ordered': bool(ts < tv < tm), 'gap_max_minus_volavg_s': tm - tv, 'tol_gap': 60.0,
                 'underestimate_by_volavg_h': (tm - tv) / P.SEC_PER_HOUR,
                 'underestimate_pct': 100.0 * (tm - tv) / tm,
                 'passed': bool(ts < tv < tm and (tm - tv) > 60.0)}
    res['criterion_comparison'] = crits

    # ---- V3.3 时间容差对照：rtol 放松一档，t_end 变化必须 < 60 s
    _, t_loose, _ = solve_to_event(event_max_criterion, rtol=1e-6, atol=1e-8)
    ck['V3.3'] = {'name': '时间容差对照（t_end）', 'measured_dt_end_s': abs(t_loose - t_end),
                  'tol': 60.0, 't_end_rtol_1e_6_s': t_loose, 't_end_rtol_1e_8_s': t_end,
                  'passed': bool(abs(t_loose - t_end) < 60.0)}

    # ---- V3.2 网格收敛：t_end 对网格的敏感性（五档 + Richardson 外推）
    ladder = []
    prev = None
    for n in (30, 60, 120, 240, 480):
        _, t_n, _ = solve_to_event(event_max_criterion, N=n)
        rec = {'N': n, 't_end_s': t_n, 't_end_h': t_n / P.SEC_PER_HOUR}
        if prev is not None:
            rec['dt_end_vs_prev_s'] = abs(t_n - prev)
            rec['dt_end_vs_prev_rel'] = abs(t_n - prev) / t_n
        prev = t_n
        ladder.append(rec)
    dseq = [r['dt_end_vs_prev_s'] for r in ladder if 'dt_end_vs_prev_s' in r]
    rseq = [r['dt_end_vs_prev_rel'] for r in ladder if 'dt_end_vs_prev_rel' in r]
    order = float(np.log2(dseq[-2] / dseq[-1])) if dseq[-1] > 0 else float('nan')
    rich = ladder[-1]['t_end_s'] - dseq[-1] / (2.0 ** order - 1.0)
    ck['V3.2'] = {'name': '网格收敛阶梯（t_end，五档）', 'dt_end_seq_s': dseq,
                  'dt_end_seq_rel': rseq, 'finest_pair_s': dseq[-1], 'finest_pair_rel': rseq[-1],
                  'tol_rel': 2e-3, 'observed_order': order,
                  'richardson_limit_s': float(rich), 'richardson_limit_h': float(rich / P.SEC_PER_HOUR),
                  'baseline_minus_richardson_h': (t_end - rich) / P.SEC_PER_HOUR,
                  'monotone_decreasing': bool(all(dseq[i] > dseq[i + 1] for i in range(len(dseq) - 1))),
                  'note': 't_end 以约一阶精度收敛（表面重构与阈值定位均为一阶）；最细两档相对变化 '
                          '%.3f%%，故用相对判据 0.2%%' % (100 * rseq[-1]),
                  'passed': bool(rseq[-1] < 2e-3
                                 and all(dseq[i] > dseq[i + 1] for i in range(len(dseq) - 1)))}
    res['mesh_ladder'] = ladder

    # ---- XA2 与问题二逐格恒等：同一右端 + 同一积分设定必须逐位相同
    q2_sol, q2_cfg = Q2.solve(t_eval=P.Q2_TABLE_TIMES)
    same = U.integrate(rhs_coupled_q3, (0.0, float(P.Q2_T_END)), U.initial_state(N),
                       t_eval=P.Q2_TABLE_TIMES, N=N, max_step=P.MAX_STEP_SHORT)
    xa2 = float(np.abs(same.y - q2_sol.y).max())
    mine = np.column_stack([sol.sol(t) for t in P.Q2_TABLE_TIMES])
    long_dev = np.abs(mine - q2_sol.y)
    ck['XA2'] = {'name': '问题三与问题二逐格恒等（t<=10800 s）', 'measured_max_abs_diff': xa2,
                 'tol': 1e-9, 'n_times': len(P.Q2_TABLE_TIMES), 'n_cells': 2 * N,
                 'long_run_max_abs_dT_C': float(long_dev[:N].max()),
                 'long_run_max_abs_dC': float(long_dev[N:].max()),
                 'note': '同一右端、同一 max_step 时逐位相同；长时程运行用 max_step=60 s（M6），'
                         '其与问题二的差异 4.4e-06 °C 纯属时间步差异，远小于四位小数口径',
                 'passed': bool(xa2 < 1e-9)}

    # ---- V3.12 量级对照：题面"一般持续 2-3 天"
    d = t_end / (24 * P.SEC_PER_HOUR)
    ck['V3.12'] = {'name': '与题面 2-3 天量级对照', 'measured_days': d,
                   'reference_range_days': [P.DUR_DAYS_LOW, P.DUR_DAYS_HIGH],
                   'inside_range': bool(P.DUR_DAYS_LOW <= d <= P.DUR_DAYS_HIGH),
                   'same_order_of_magnitude': bool(0.1 * P.DUR_DAYS_LOW <= d <= 10 * P.DUR_DAYS_HIGH),
                   'note': '题面区间是独立于模型的量级参照，不是标定目标；未对任何物性做反向调参',
                   'passed': bool(0.1 * P.DUR_DAYS_LOW <= d <= 10 * P.DUR_DAYS_HIGH)}

    # ---- 输出网格：60 s 步长直到 t_end（result3 只含水分浓度，见 O6）
    n_rows = int(np.floor(t_end / P.OUT_STEP_LONG))
    times = [P.OUT_STEP_LONG * (k + 1) for k in range(n_rows)]
    Cg = np.empty((len(times), len(P.OUT_R_CM)))
    Tg = np.empty_like(Cg)
    cmax_series = np.empty(len(times))
    volavg_series = np.empty(len(times))
    for k, t in enumerate(times):
        y = sol.sol(float(t))
        T, C = y[:N], y[N:]
        T_s, C_s = U.surface_pair(cfg, T, C, float(t))[:2]
        Tg[k] = U.to_output_radii(T, T_s, cfg['eta_c'], P.OUT_R_CM, P.R0_CM)
        Cg[k] = U.to_output_radii(C, C_s, cfg['eta_c'], P.OUT_R_CM, P.R0_CM)
        cmax_series[k] = C.max()
        volavg_series[k] = U.vol_average(C, cfg['V_cell'])
    rt = U.write_result_xlsx(P.RESULT_FILES[3], {'Sheet1': Cg}, times, P.OUT_R_CM)
    ck['C16'] = {'name': '写盘-读回往返一致',
                 'measured': max(v['roundtrip_max_abs_diff'] for v in rt.values()),
                 'tol': 0.0, 'per_sheet': rt, 'passed': True}

    # ---- 第 2 层健康性（长时程）
    ck['C13'] = {'name': '干燥方向性（全程）', 'max_rise_of_Cmax': float(np.max(np.diff(cmax_series))),
                 'max_rise_of_volavg': float(np.max(np.diff(volavg_series))), 'tol': 1e-12,
                 'passed': bool(np.max(np.diff(cmax_series)) < 1e-12
                                and np.max(np.diff(volavg_series)) < 1e-12)}
    ta_env = np.array([U.driver_Ta(t) for t in times])
    lo, hi = min(P.T0_C, ta_env.min(), U.TA_PLATEAU), max(P.T0_C, ta_env.max(), U.TA_PLATEAU)
    ck['V2.4'] = {'name': '温度落在驱动包络内', 'T_min': float(Tg.min()), 'T_max': float(Tg.max()),
                  'envelope': [float(lo), float(hi)],
                  'passed': bool(Tg.min() >= lo - 1e-9 and Tg.max() <= hi + 1e-9)}

    # ---- 论文表 5：每隔 6 h x 每隔 0.5 cm，末行给出烘干结束时间
    six_h = int(6 * P.SEC_PER_HOUR)
    t5 = [six_h * k for k in range(1, int(t_end // six_h) + 1)]
    cols = [P.OUT_R_CM.index(r) for r in P.TABLE_R_CM]
    rows5 = [[float(x) for x in np.round(U.to_output_radii(
        sol.sol(float(t))[N:], U.surface_pair(cfg, sol.sol(float(t))[:N], sol.sol(float(t))[N:],
                                             float(t))[1], cfg['eta_c'], P.OUT_R_CM, P.R0_CM)[cols], 4)]
        for t in t5]
    # 末行取事件时刻的场值（sol.y_events），不得用 60 s 输出网格的末行冒充 t_end
    y_end = sol.y_events[0][0]
    C_s_end = U.surface_pair(cfg, y_end[:N], y_end[N:], t_end)[1]
    row_end = [float(x) for x in np.round(U.to_output_radii(
        y_end[N:], C_s_end, cfg['eta_c'], P.OUT_R_CM, P.R0_CM)[cols], 4)]
    res['table_5_moisture'] = {'times_s': t5, 'times_h': [t / P.SEC_PER_HOUR for t in t5],
                               'radii_cm': P.TABLE_R_CM, 'values': rows5,
                               't_end_row': {'label': '烘干结束时间', 'hours': t_end / P.SEC_PER_HOUR,
                                             'seconds': t_end, 'values': row_end,
                                             'source': 'sol.y_events at t_end'}}
    res['summary'] = {'t_end_h': t_end / P.SEC_PER_HOUR, 't_end_days': d,
                      'C_max_at_t_end': float(sol.y_events[0][0][N:].max()),
                      'C_center_at_t_end': float(sol.y_events[0][0][N:][0]),
                      'C_surface_at_t_end': float(U.surface_pair(
                          cfg, sol.y_events[0][0][:N], sol.y_events[0][0][N:], t_end)[1]),
                      'volavg_C_at_t_end': float(U.vol_average(sol.y_events[0][0][N:], cfg['V_cell'])),
                      'T_center_at_t_end': float(sol.y_events[0][0][0]),
                      'herb_T_max': float(Tg.max()), 'herb_T_min': float(Tg.min()),
                      'herb_C_max': float(Cg.max()), 'herb_C_min': float(Cg.min()),
                      'n_output_rows': len(times)}
    res['drying_curve'] = {'t_s': times[::10], 't_h': [t / P.SEC_PER_HOUR for t in times[::10]],
                           'C_max': np.round(cmax_series[::10], 6).tolist(),
                           'C_volavg': np.round(volavg_series[::10], 6).tolist(),
                           'C_center': np.round(Cg[::10, 0], 6).tolist(),
                           'C_surface': np.round(Cg[::10, -1], 6).tolist(),
                           'T_center': np.round(Tg[::10, 0], 6).tolist(),
                           'threshold': P.C_THR}
    res['heatmap'] = {'t_s': times[::20], 't_h': [t / P.SEC_PER_HOUR for t in times[::20]],
                      'r_cm': P.OUT_R_CM, 'C': np.round(Cg[::20], 4).tolist(),
                      'T': np.round(Tg[::20], 4).tolist()}
    res['profiles'] = {'times_s': t5, 'r_cm': P.OUT_R_CM,
                       'C': [[float(x) for x in np.round(U.to_output_radii(
                           sol.sol(float(t))[N:], U.surface_pair(
                               cfg, sol.sol(float(t))[:N], sol.sol(float(t))[N:], float(t))[1],
                           cfg['eta_c'], P.OUT_R_CM, P.R0_CM), 6)] for t in t5]}
    res['xlsx_report'] = rt
    res['checks'] = ck
    res['all_checks_passed'] = bool(all(v.get('passed') for v in ck.values()))
    return res, ck, sol, cfg, t_end, times, Cg, Tg


if __name__ == '__main__':
    out = run()[0]
    P.FIG_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    (P.FIG_DIR / 'problem_3_results.json').write_text(
        json.dumps(out, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding='utf-8')
    for k, v in out['checks'].items():
        print(f"{k}: {'PASS' if v.get('passed') else 'FAIL'}  {v.get('name', '')}")
    print('Q3 checks passed:', out['all_checks_passed'])
    print('t_end = %.3f s = %.4f h = %.4f d' % (out['t_end']['seconds'], out['t_end']['hours'],
                                                out['t_end']['days']))
    c = out['criterion_comparison']
    print('criteria h: surface %.4f | volavg %.4f | max %.4f'
          % (c['surface']['t_end_h'], c['volavg']['t_end_h'], c['max']['t_end_h']))
```

**B.7　problem4.py**

```python
# code/problem4.py — 问题四：径向收缩下的移动边界烘干（附录 4 物性 + 附件 2 半径轨迹）
import json

import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))   # 本机为嵌入式发行版，不自动加脚本目录

import params as P
import utils as U

PROPS_APPENDIX = 'appendix_4'
SHRINK_MODE = 'radial'            # ③ 方向性判定：只径向收缩，轴向 25 cm 不变（见 C_direction）
CFG = U.make_config(PROPS_APPENDIX)
C20_T_END = 43200.0               # C20 守恒试验时长（12 h，R 由 2.0000 收缩到 1.2480 cm）


def rhs_coupled_q4(t, y):
    """问题四右端：附录 4 变物性 + 材料坐标收缩域（④M11，收缩只经 1/R(t)^2 进入）。"""
    return U.moving_domain_rhs(t, y, CFG)


def water_mass_per_length(t, C, V_cell):
    """单位长度含水质量 m_w = 2*pi*rho_d(t)*R(t)**2*sum(V_i C_i)（kg/m）。

    rho_d(t)*R(t)**2 = rho_d0*R0**2 恒定，故 h_m=0 时 m_w 守恒等价于 sum(V_i C_i) 守恒。
    """
    R_m = U.R_of_t(t)
    return 2.0 * np.pi * U.rho_d_of_t(t) * R_m ** 2 * float(np.asarray(C) @ V_cell)


def solve_case(props_key=PROPS_APPENDIX, moving=True, N=P.N_CELLS, dense=False,
               rtol=None, atol=None):
    """统一入口：按 (物性附录, 是否收缩) 积分到全域最大含水率首次下穿阈值。"""
    cfg = CFG if (props_key == PROPS_APPENDIX and N == P.N_CELLS) else U.make_config(props_key, N=N)
    if moving:
        rhs = (rhs_coupled_q4 if cfg is CFG else (lambda t, y: U.moving_domain_rhs(t, y, cfg)))
    else:
        rhs = (lambda t, y: U.fixed_domain_rhs(t, y, cfg))
    kw = dict(events=U.event_moisture_threshold, max_step=P.MAX_STEP_LONG, dense_output=dense)
    if rtol is None:
        sol = U.integrate(rhs, (0.0, P.T_INTEGRATION_CAP), U.initial_state(cfg['N']),
                          N=cfg['N'], **kw)
    else:
        import scipy.integrate as si
        sol = si.solve_ivp(rhs, (0.0, P.T_INTEGRATION_CAP), U.initial_state(cfg['N']),
                           method='BDF', rtol=rtol, atol=atol,
                           jac_sparsity=U.jacobian_sparsity(cfg['N']), **kw)
    assert sol.success, sol.message
    assert len(sol.t_events[0]) > 0, '事件未触发：t_end 超出保护上限 %g s' % P.T_INTEGRATION_CAP
    return sol, float(sol.t_events[0][0]), cfg


# ------------------------------------------------------------ 第 1 层单元测试
def unit_tests():
    ck = {}
    N = CFG['N']
    ck['C11'] = {'name': '附录绑定（附录 4 物性 + 附件 2 半径）', 'props_appendix': PROPS_APPENDIX,
                 'shrink_mode': SHRINK_MODE, 'passed': PROPS_APPENDIX == 'appendix_4'}

    # C18 半径轨迹：单调不增（含导数）且逐点复现附件 2
    ts = np.linspace(0.0, U.R_T_LAST, 10000)
    Rv = np.array([U.R_of_t(float(t)) for t in ts]) * P.CM_PER_M
    dRv = np.array([U.dRdt(float(t)) for t in ts]) * P.CM_PER_M
    repro = max(abs(U.R_of_t(float(t)) * P.CM_PER_M - r)
                for t, r in zip(U.R_T_GRID, U.R_DATA_CM))
    ck['C18'] = {'name': '半径轨迹单调不增且复现附件 2',
                 'max_dR_over_scan_cm': float(np.max(np.diff(Rv))),
                 'max_dRdt_cm_per_s': float(dRv.max()), 'min_dRdt_cm_per_s': float(dRv.min()),
                 'n_scan_points': int(ts.size), 'sample_repro_max_abs_cm': float(repro),
                 'tol_repro_cm': 1e-12, 'R0_cm': float(Rv[0]), 'R_end_cm': float(Rv[-1]),
                 'total_shrink_pct': float(100.0 * (1.0 - Rv[-1] / Rv[0])),
                 'passed': bool(np.max(np.diff(Rv)) <= 1e-14 and dRv.max() <= 1e-14
                                and repro < 1e-12)}

    # C20 绝干骨架守恒：h_m=0 时 sum(V_i C_i) 逐位守恒；三个反向变体必须显著漂移
    cfg0 = U.make_config(PROPS_APPENDIX, h_m=0.0)
    V = cfg0['V_cell']
    y0 = np.concatenate((np.full(N, P.T0_C), P.C0 * (0.6 + 0.8 * cfg0['eta_c'] ** 2)))
    m0 = float(y0[N:] @ V)
    drift, s_fwd = {}, None
    for tag, var in (('forward_M11', None), ('reverse_B_spurious_advection', 'spurious_advection'),
                     ('reverse_C_nonconservative', 'nonconservative')):
        s = U.integrate(lambda t, y, v=var: U.moving_domain_rhs(t, y, cfg0, v),
                        (0.0, C20_T_END), y0, N=N, max_step=P.MAX_STEP_LONG)
        assert s.success, s.message
        drift[tag] = abs(float(s.y[N:, -1] @ V) - m0) / m0
        if var is None:
            s_fwd = s
    m_end = float(s_fwd.y[N:, -1] @ V)
    R_a, R_b = U.R_of_t(0.0), U.R_of_t(C20_T_END)
    drift['reverse_A_drop_rho_d'] = abs(R_b ** 2 * m_end / (R_a ** 2 * m0) - 1.0)
    keep = abs(water_mass_per_length(C20_T_END, s_fwd.y[N:, -1], V)
               / water_mass_per_length(0.0, y0[N:], V) - 1.0)
    rev = [drift[k] for k in drift if k.startswith('reverse_')]
    ck['C20'] = {'name': '绝干骨架水量守恒（正向 + 三个反向变体）',
                 'forward_rel_drift': drift['forward_M11'], 'tol_forward': 1e-8,
                 'mass_kg_per_m_rel_drift_with_bookkeeping': float(keep),
                 'reverse_rel_drift': {k: v for k, v in drift.items() if k.startswith('reverse_')},
                 'tol_reverse_min': 1e-4, 'min_reverse_drift': float(min(rev)),
                 't_test_s': C20_T_END, 'R_cm_span': [R_a * P.CM_PER_M, R_b * P.CM_PER_M],
                 'rho_d_span': [U.rho_d_of_t(0.0), U.rho_d_of_t(C20_T_END)],
                 'note': '反向 A 丢弃 rho_d(t) 记账、B 按 §7.5 再加 R*Rdot 对流通量、'
                         'C 用非守恒 Laplacian 展开；三者分别漂移 %.2f%%、%.2f%%、%.2f%%'
                         % (100 * drift['reverse_A_drop_rho_d'],
                            100 * drift['reverse_B_spurious_advection'],
                            100 * drift['reverse_C_nonconservative']),
                 'passed': bool(drift['forward_M11'] < 1e-8 and min(rev) > 1e-4)}

    # C22 t=0 退化：R(0)=R0 时收缩域右端必须与固定域右端逐位相同
    y_init = U.initial_state(N)
    d0 = float(np.abs(U.moving_domain_rhs(0.0, y_init, CFG)
                      - U.fixed_domain_rhs(0.0, y_init, CFG)).max())
    ck['C22'] = {'name': 't=0 收缩域退化为固定域', 'measured_max_abs_diff': d0, 'tol': 1e-12,
                 'R_at_0_cm': U.R_of_t(0.0) * P.CM_PER_M, 'dRdt_at_0_cm_per_s': U.dRdt(0.0),
                 'passed': bool(d0 < 1e-12)}

    # 方向性判定：径向收缩下的末态绝干密度须与附录 4 零含水率密度相容，各向同性则不相容
    rho_radial = U.RHO_D0 * (P.R0_CM / U.R_MIN_CM) ** 2
    rho_iso = U.RHO_D0 * (P.R0_CM / U.R_MIN_CM) ** 3
    dev_rad = 100.0 * (rho_radial - P.RHO4_A) / P.RHO4_A
    dev_iso = 100.0 * (rho_iso - P.RHO4_A) / P.RHO4_A
    ck['C_direction'] = {'name': '收缩方向性判定（径向 vs 各向同性）', 'rho_d0': U.RHO_D0,
                         'rho_d_end_radial': float(rho_radial), 'rho_d_end_isotropic': float(rho_iso),
                         'reference_rho_zero_moisture': P.RHO4_A,
                         'deviation_radial_pct': float(dev_rad),
                         'deviation_isotropic_pct': float(dev_iso), 'tol_accept_pct': 5.0,
                         'tol_reject_pct': 20.0, 'selected': SHRINK_MODE,
                         'note': '径向假设下末态绝干密度 %.2f kg/m^3 与附录 4 零含水率密度 %g 相差 '
                                 '%.2f%%；各向同性假设给出 %.2f kg/m^3，相差 %.2f%%，物理上不可接受'
                                 % (rho_radial, P.RHO4_A, dev_rad, rho_iso, dev_iso),
                         'passed': bool(abs(dev_rad) < 5.0 and abs(dev_iso) > 20.0)}

    # 收缩量自洽性：几何体积比 vs 由附录 4 密度式反推的质量体积比（如实披露差距）
    rho_wet_thr = P.RHO4_A + P.RHO4_B * P.C_THR
    rho_d_thr = rho_wet_thr / (1.0 + P.C_THR)
    vr_mass = U.RHO_D0 / rho_d_thr
    vr_geom = (U.R_MIN_CM / P.R0_CM) ** 2
    gap = abs(vr_mass - vr_geom) / vr_mass
    ck['C_volume_consistency'] = {'name': '收缩量自洽性（几何 vs 密度式反推）',
                                  'volume_ratio_geometric': float(vr_geom),
                                  'volume_ratio_from_density': float(vr_mass),
                                  'relative_gap': float(gap), 'tol': 0.20,
                                  'rho_d_at_C_thr': float(rho_d_thr),
                                  'note': '附件 2 的几何收缩量 %.4f 与附录 4 密度式反推的 %.4f 差 %.2f%%；'
                                          '两者同阶但不完全自洽，本文以附件 2 实测轨迹为准，未反向调参'
                                          % (vr_geom, vr_mass, 100 * gap),
                                  'passed': bool(gap < 0.20)}
    return ck


# ------------------------------------------------------------ 主求解
def run():
    res = {'problem': 4, 'props_appendix': PROPS_APPENDIX, 'shrink_mode': SHRINK_MODE}
    ck = unit_tests()
    sol, t_end, cfg = solve_case(dense=True)
    N = cfg['N']
    res['t_end'] = {'seconds': t_end, 'hours': t_end / P.SEC_PER_HOUR,
                    'days': t_end / (24 * P.SEC_PER_HOUR), 'nfev': int(sol.nfev),
                    'n_solver_steps': int(len(sol.t)),
                    'R_at_t_end_cm': U.R_of_t(t_end) * P.CM_PER_M}

    # C19 首次下穿：事件时刻贴阈值，且此前密扫无更早穿越
    y_e = sol.y_events[0][0]
    C_e = y_e[N:]
    hit = abs(float(C_e.max()) - P.C_THR)
    scan_t = np.linspace(0.0, t_end * (1 - 1e-9), 20000)
    Cmax_scan = np.array([sol.sol(t)[N:].max() for t in scan_t])
    below = int((Cmax_scan < P.C_THR).sum())
    ck['C19'] = {'name': '首次下穿（密扫无更早穿越）', 'measured_endpoint_gap': float(hit),
                 'tol': 1e-5, 'n_scan_points': int(scan_t.size), 'n_points_below_thr': below,
                 'min_Cmax_before_t_end': float(Cmax_scan.min()),
                 'passed': bool(hit < 1e-5 and below == 0)}
    ck['C07'] = {'name': '事件时刻最大值在中心', 'argmax_cell': int(C_e.argmax()),
                 'C_center': float(C_e[0]), 'C_outermost_cell': float(C_e[-1]),
                 'passed': bool(C_e.argmax() == 0)}

    # ---- C21 归因分解：2x2 四次独立真实运行（物性附录 x 是否收缩）
    runs, sols = {}, {}
    for tag, key, mv in (('q3_baseline', 'appendix_3', False), ('app4_noshrink', 'appendix_4', False),
                         ('app3_shrink', 'appendix_3', True), ('app4_shrink', 'appendix_4', True)):
        s_c, t_c, _ = solve_case(key, mv, dense=(tag == 'q3_baseline'))
        runs[tag] = {'props': key, 'shrink': mv, 't_end_s': t_c,
                     't_end_h': t_c / P.SEC_PER_HOUR, 'nfev': int(s_c.nfev)}
        sols[tag] = s_c
    t_b, t_p, t_s, t_f = (runs[k]['t_end_s'] for k in
                          ('q3_baseline', 'app4_noshrink', 'app3_shrink', 'app4_shrink'))
    d_props, d_shrink = t_p - t_b, t_f - t_p
    # 收缩效应依所在物性基准而异：附录 4 上为 d_shrink，附录 3 上为 d_shrink_app3
    d_shrink_app3 = t_s - t_b
    # 物性效应同样有两个基准：固定域上为 d_props，收缩域上为 d_props_shrunk
    d_props_shrunk = t_f - t_s
    inter = d_shrink - d_shrink_app3
    prod = (t_p / t_b) * (t_s / t_b)
    # 2x2 设计只有 4 个数、4 个待定量（基准 + 两个主效应 + 交互），是饱和设计：
    # 任何"链式闭合"都是望远镜恒等式，恒过而无信息量，故不作为校验项。
    # 可失败的判据有两条：四次运行必须两两可分辨；乘性框架的残差必须真的小。
    t_all = sorted((t_b, t_p, t_s, t_f))
    min_gap = min(t_all[i + 1] - t_all[i] for i in range(3))
    mult_resid = 100.0 * abs((t_f / t_b) / prod - 1.0)
    ck['C21'] = {'name': '归因分解四例可分辨与乘性框架残差',
                 'min_pairwise_gap_s': float(min_gap), 'tol_min_gap_s': 60.0,
                 'tol_mult_residual_pct': 20.0,
                 'delta_props_s': d_props, 'delta_shrink_s': d_shrink,
                 'delta_shrink_on_app3_s': float(d_shrink_app3),
                 'delta_props_on_shrunk_s': float(d_props_shrunk),
                 'total_s': t_f - t_b, 'interaction_s': float(inter),
                 'interaction_over_total_pct': float(100.0 * abs(inter) / abs(t_f - t_b)),
                 'mult_ratio_props': t_p / t_b, 'mult_ratio_shrink': t_s / t_b,
                 'mult_predicted_ratio': float(prod), 'mult_actual_ratio': t_f / t_b,
                 'mult_residual_pct': float(100.0 * abs((t_f / t_b) / prod - 1.0)),
                 'n_runs': len(runs),
                 'note': '收缩效应在附录 3 物性下为 %.0f s、在附录 4 物性下为 %.0f s，二者之差 %.0f s 即交互项；'
                         '物性效应在固定域为 %.0f s、在收缩域为 %.0f s。加法框架下交互项达总效应的 %.0f%%，'
                         '乘法框架残差 %.1f%%（以预测比值为分母），故两机制近似乘性叠加'
                         % (d_shrink_app3, d_shrink, inter, d_props, d_props_shrunk,
                            100.0 * abs(inter) / abs(t_f - t_b),
                            100.0 * abs((t_f / t_b) / prod - 1.0)),
                 'passed': bool(len(runs) == 4 and min_gap > 60.0 and mult_resid < 20.0)}
    res['attribution'] = runs

    # ---- V3.2 网格收敛（五档 + Richardson 外推，t_end 以相对判据考核）
    ladder, prev = [], None
    for n in (30, 60, 120, 240, 480):
        _, t_n, _ = solve_case(N=n)
        rec = {'N': n, 't_end_s': t_n, 't_end_h': t_n / P.SEC_PER_HOUR}
        if prev is not None:
            rec['dt_end_vs_prev_s'] = abs(t_n - prev)
            rec['dt_end_vs_prev_rel'] = abs(t_n - prev) / t_n
        prev = t_n
        ladder.append(rec)
    dseq = [r['dt_end_vs_prev_s'] for r in ladder if 'dt_end_vs_prev_s' in r]
    rseq = [r['dt_end_vs_prev_rel'] for r in ladder if 'dt_end_vs_prev_rel' in r]
    order = float(np.log2(dseq[-2] / dseq[-1])) if dseq[-1] > 0 else float('nan')
    rich = ladder[-1]['t_end_s'] - dseq[-1] / (2.0 ** order - 1.0)
    ck['V3.2'] = {'name': '网格收敛阶梯（t_end，五档）', 'dt_end_seq_s': dseq,
                  'dt_end_seq_rel': rseq, 'finest_pair_s': dseq[-1], 'finest_pair_rel': rseq[-1],
                  'tol_rel': 2e-3, 'observed_order': order, 'richardson_limit_s': float(rich),
                  'richardson_limit_h': float(rich / P.SEC_PER_HOUR),
                  'baseline_minus_richardson_h': (t_end - rich) / P.SEC_PER_HOUR,
                  'monotone_decreasing': bool(all(dseq[i] > dseq[i + 1] for i in range(len(dseq) - 1))),
                  'note': '与问题三同口径：表面重构与阈值定位均为一阶，故 t_end 用相对判据 0.2%%，'
                          '最细两档相对变化 %.3f%%' % (100 * rseq[-1]),
                  'passed': bool(rseq[-1] < 2e-3
                                 and all(dseq[i] > dseq[i + 1] for i in range(len(dseq) - 1)))}
    res['mesh_ladder'] = ladder

    # ---- V3.3 时间容差对照
    _, t_loose, _ = solve_case(rtol=1e-6, atol=1e-8)
    ck['V3.3'] = {'name': '时间容差对照（t_end）', 'measured_dt_end_s': abs(t_loose - t_end),
                  'tol': 60.0, 't_end_rtol_1e_6_s': t_loose, 't_end_rtol_1e_8_s': t_end,
                  'passed': bool(abs(t_loose - t_end) < 60.0)}

    # ---- 输出网格：60 s 步长到 t_end；r > R(t) 的格写空（③P09），末列为药材表面
    n_rows = int(np.floor(t_end / P.OUT_STEP_LONG))
    times = [P.OUT_STEP_LONG * (k + 1) for k in range(n_rows)]
    r_fixed = np.asarray(P.Q4_FIXED_R_CM, float)
    Cg = np.full((len(times), r_fixed.size + 1), np.nan)
    Tg = np.full_like(Cg, np.nan)
    R_ser = np.empty(len(times))
    Cs_ser = np.empty(len(times))
    Ts_ser = np.empty(len(times))
    cmax_ser = np.empty(len(times))
    vavg_ser = np.empty(len(times))
    mw_ser = np.empty(len(times))
    for k, t in enumerate(times):
        y = sol.sol(float(t))
        T, C = y[:N], y[N:]
        R_m = U.R_of_t(float(t))
        R_cm = R_m * P.CM_PER_M
        T_s, C_s = U.surface_pair(cfg, T, C, float(t), R_m=R_m)[:2]
        ins = r_fixed <= R_cm + 1e-12
        Cg[k, :-1][ins] = U.to_output_radii(C, C_s, cfg['eta_c'], r_fixed[ins], R_cm)
        Tg[k, :-1][ins] = U.to_output_radii(T, T_s, cfg['eta_c'], r_fixed[ins], R_cm)
        Cg[k, -1], Tg[k, -1] = C_s, T_s
        R_ser[k], Cs_ser[k], Ts_ser[k] = R_cm, C_s, T_s
        cmax_ser[k] = C.max()
        vavg_ser[k] = U.vol_average(C, cfg['V_cell'])
        mw_ser[k] = water_mass_per_length(float(t), C, cfg['V_cell'])
    cols = [*P.Q4_FIXED_R_CM, P.SURFACE_LABEL]
    rt = U.write_result_xlsx(P.RESULT_FILES[4], {'Sheet1': Cg}, times, cols)
    ck['C16'] = {'name': '写盘-读回往返一致（含空单元格掩码）',
                 'measured': max(v['roundtrip_max_abs_diff'] for v in rt.values()),
                 'tol': 0.0, 'per_sheet': rt, 'n_blank_cells': rt['Sheet1']['blank_cells'],
                 'blank_rule': 'r > R(t) 的格为空（药材已收缩到该位置以内）', 'passed': True}

    # ---- 全局水量平衡闭合：储量减少量 = 表面对流累计失水量
    tb = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.asarray(times, float)))
    y_0 = U.initial_state(N)
    mw_b = np.concatenate(([water_mass_per_length(0.0, y_0[N:], cfg['V_cell'])], mw_ser))
    cs_b = np.concatenate(([U.surface_pair(cfg, y_0[:N], y_0[N:], 0.0, R_m=U.R_of_t(0.0))[1]], Cs_ser))
    ca_b = np.array([U.driver_Ca(float(t)) for t in tb])
    rate = np.array([2.0 * np.pi * U.R_of_t(float(t)) * U.rho_d_of_t(float(t)) * P.HM_MASS
                     for t in tb]) * (cs_b - ca_b)
    cum = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(0.5 * (rate[1:] + rate[:-1]) * np.diff(tb))))
    drop = mw_b[0] - mw_b[-1]
    closure = abs(drop - cum[-1]) / drop
    ck['C20_balance'] = {'name': '全局水量平衡闭合（h_m 非零）', 'measured_rel_gap': float(closure),
                         'tol': 1e-3, 'stored_drop_kg_per_m': float(drop),
                         'cumulative_flux_kg_per_m': float(cum[-1]),
                         'm_w_initial_kg_per_m': float(mw_b[0]), 'm_w_final_kg_per_m': float(mw_b[-1]),
                         'note': '储量按 2*pi*rho_d(t)*R(t)^2*sum(V_i C_i) 记，失水按表面对流通量'
                                 '在 60 s 网格上梯形累加，残差含梯形求积误差',
                         'passed': bool(closure < 1e-3)}

    # ---- 第 2 层健康性
    ck['C13'] = {'name': '干燥方向性（全程）', 'max_rise_of_Cmax': float(np.max(np.diff(cmax_ser))),
                 'max_rise_of_volavg': float(np.max(np.diff(vavg_ser))), 'tol': 1e-12,
                 'passed': bool(np.max(np.diff(cmax_ser)) < 1e-12
                                and np.max(np.diff(vavg_ser)) < 1e-12)}
    ta_env = np.array([U.driver_Ta(float(t)) for t in times])
    lo = min(P.T0_C, ta_env.min(), U.TA_PLATEAU)
    hi = max(P.T0_C, ta_env.max(), U.TA_PLATEAU)
    ck['V2.4'] = {'name': '温度落在驱动包络内', 'T_min': float(np.nanmin(Tg)),
                  'T_max': float(np.nanmax(Tg)), 'envelope': [float(lo), float(hi)],
                  'passed': bool(np.nanmin(Tg) >= lo - 1e-9 and np.nanmax(Tg) <= hi + 1e-9)}
    ck['V3.12'] = {'name': '与题面 2-3 天量级对照', 'measured_days': t_end / (24 * P.SEC_PER_HOUR),
                   'reference_range_days': [P.DUR_DAYS_LOW, P.DUR_DAYS_HIGH],
                   'inside_range': bool(P.DUR_DAYS_LOW <= t_end / (24 * P.SEC_PER_HOUR)
                                        <= P.DUR_DAYS_HIGH),
                   'note': '量级参照，非标定目标',
                   'passed': bool(0.1 * P.DUR_DAYS_LOW <= t_end / (24 * P.SEC_PER_HOUR)
                                  <= 10 * P.DUR_DAYS_HIGH)}

    def snap(t, r_req):
        """给定时刻在请求半径列上的 (T, C)；超出当前外表面的位置返回 nan。"""
        y = sol.sol(float(t))
        T, C = y[:N], y[N:]
        R_m = U.R_of_t(float(t))
        R_cm = R_m * P.CM_PER_M
        T_s, C_s = U.surface_pair(cfg, T, C, float(t), R_m=R_m)[:2]
        rr = np.asarray(r_req, float)
        ins = rr <= R_cm + 1e-12
        tv = np.full(rr.size, np.nan)
        cv = np.full(rr.size, np.nan)
        tv[ins] = U.to_output_radii(T, T_s, cfg['eta_c'], rr[ins], R_cm)
        cv[ins] = U.to_output_radii(C, C_s, cfg['eta_c'], rr[ins], R_cm)
        return tv, cv, R_cm, T_s, C_s

    def jnan(a):
        return [None if not np.isfinite(x) else float(x) for x in np.asarray(a, float)]

    # ---- 论文表 6：每隔 6 h x 每隔 0.5 cm，附半径与表面值
    six_h = int(6 * P.SEC_PER_HOUR)
    t6 = [six_h * k for k in range(1, int(t_end // six_h) + 1)]
    rows6 = []
    for t in t6:
        _, cv, R_cm, _, C_s = snap(t, P.TABLE_R_CM)
        rows6.append({'t_s': t, 't_h': t / P.SEC_PER_HOUR, 'R_cm': round(R_cm, 4),
                      'C': jnan(np.round(cv, 4)), 'C_surface': round(float(C_s), 4)})
    # 末行取事件时刻的场值，与上面各行同一 snap 口径；不得用 60 s 输出网格末行冒充 t_end
    _, cv_end, R_cm_end, _, C_s_end = snap(t_end, P.TABLE_R_CM)
    res['table_6_moisture'] = {'radii_cm': P.TABLE_R_CM, 'rows': rows6,
                               'surface_label': P.SURFACE_LABEL,
                               't_end_row': {'label': '烘干结束时间', 'hours': t_end / P.SEC_PER_HOUR,
                                             'seconds': t_end, 'R_cm': U.R_of_t(t_end) * P.CM_PER_M,
                                             'C': jnan(np.round(cv_end, 4)),
                                             'C_surface': round(float(C_s_end), 4),
                                             'source': 'sol.sol(t_end) at event time'}}

    # ---- 图件数据：半径轨迹、收缩自洽、移动边界剖面、与问题三对比、水量平衡、热图
    t_dense = np.linspace(0.0, U.R_T_LAST, 601)
    res['radius_curve'] = {'t_data_s': U.R_T_GRID.tolist(), 'R_data_cm': U.R_DATA_CM.tolist(),
                           't_dense_s': t_dense.tolist(),
                           'R_pchip_cm': [U.R_of_t(float(t)) * P.CM_PER_M for t in t_dense],
                           'R_weibull_cm': [float(x) for x in radius_weibull_reference(t_dense)],
                           'dRdt_cm_per_s': [U.dRdt(float(t)) * P.CM_PER_M for t in t_dense],
                           't_end_s': t_end, 'R_at_t_end_cm': U.R_of_t(t_end) * P.CM_PER_M}
    res['radius_curve'].update(
        {'weibull_params': {'R_inf_cm': float(radius_weibull_reference.params[0]),
                            'tau_s': float(radius_weibull_reference.params[1]),
                            'k': float(radius_weibull_reference.params[2])},
         'weibull_rmse_cm': radius_weibull_reference.rmse_cm,
         'weibull_max_abs_cm': radius_weibull_reference.max_abs_cm,
         'note': 'Weibull 只作描述性参考；PDE 中的 R(t) 一律取附件 2 的保单调 PCHIP 插值'})
    res['shrink_consistency'] = {k: ck['C_volume_consistency'][k] for k in
                                 ('volume_ratio_geometric', 'volume_ratio_from_density',
                                  'relative_gap', 'rho_d_at_C_thr')}
    res['shrink_consistency'].update({'rho_d0': U.RHO_D0,
                                      'rho_d_end_radial': ck['C_direction']['rho_d_end_radial'],
                                      'rho_d_end_isotropic': ck['C_direction']['rho_d_end_isotropic'],
                                      'reference_rho_zero_moisture': P.RHO4_A})

    # Bland-Altman 需要成对样本：对附件 2 的每个时刻各给一对体积比
    #   几何路径 x = (R(t)/R0)^2（附件 2 实测半径，轴向不变）
    #   密度路径 y = rho_d0 / rho_d(C̄(t))，其中 rho_d 由附录 4 湿密度式在**本解的体积平均含水率**上求值
    # 两条路径互相独立（一条只用附件 2 几何，一条只用附录 4 物性 + 解出的含水率），故其差值有诊断意义
    ba_t = U.R_T_GRID[U.R_T_GRID <= t_end]
    ba_x, ba_y, ba_cbar = [], [], []
    for t in ba_t:
        yv = sol.sol(float(t))
        cbar = float(U.vol_average(yv[N:], cfg['V_cell']))
        rho_d = (P.RHO4_A + P.RHO4_B * cbar) / (1.0 + cbar)
        ba_x.append(float((U.R_of_t(float(t)) * P.CM_PER_M / P.R0_CM) ** 2))
        ba_y.append(float(U.RHO_D0 / rho_d))
        ba_cbar.append(cbar)
    ba_x, ba_y = np.asarray(ba_x), np.asarray(ba_y)
    diff, mean = ba_y - ba_x, 0.5 * (ba_x + ba_y)
    bias, sd = float(diff.mean()), float(diff.std(ddof=1))
    res['shrink_consistency'].update({
        'bland_altman': {'t_s': ba_t.tolist(), 't_h': (ba_t / P.SEC_PER_HOUR).tolist(),
                         'volume_ratio_geometric': ba_x.tolist(),
                         'volume_ratio_from_density': ba_y.tolist(),
                         'volavg_C': ba_cbar,
                         'mean': mean.tolist(), 'diff': diff.tolist(),
                         'bias': bias, 'sd_diff': sd,
                         'loa_lower': bias - 1.96 * sd, 'loa_upper': bias + 1.96 * sd,
                         'n_pairs': int(ba_t.size),
                         'n_outside_loa': int(np.sum((diff < bias - 1.96 * sd)
                                                     | (diff > bias + 1.96 * sd))),
                         'note': '几何路径只用附件 2 半径，密度路径只用附录 4 密度式 + 本解体积平均含水率；'
                                 '两者独立，故偏差即两套数据源的自洽性度量，未用于反向调参'}})
    frames = []
    for t in t6:
        y = sol.sol(float(t))
        R_cm = U.R_of_t(float(t)) * P.CM_PER_M
        T_s, C_s = U.surface_pair(cfg, y[:N], y[N:], float(t), R_m=R_cm / P.CM_PER_M)[:2]
        frames.append({'t_s': t, 't_h': t / P.SEC_PER_HOUR, 'R_cm': R_cm,
                       'r_cm': np.round(np.concatenate((cfg['eta_c'] * R_cm, [R_cm])), 6).tolist(),
                       'C': np.round(np.concatenate((y[N:], [C_s])), 6).tolist(),
                       'T': np.round(np.concatenate((y[:N], [T_s])), 6).tolist()})
    res['moving_boundary_frames'] = frames
    grid = np.linspace(0.0, max(t_end, runs['q3_baseline']['t_end_s']), 400)

    def cmax_curve(s, t_stop):
        return [None if t > t_stop else float(s.sol(float(t))[N:].max()) for t in grid]

    res['compare_q3'] = {'t_s': grid.tolist(), 't_h': (grid / P.SEC_PER_HOUR).tolist(),
                         'C_max_q4_shrink': cmax_curve(sol, t_end),
                         'C_max_q3_fixed': cmax_curve(sols['q3_baseline'],
                                                      runs['q3_baseline']['t_end_s']),
                         't_end_q4_h': t_end / P.SEC_PER_HOUR,
                         't_end_q3_h': runs['q3_baseline']['t_end_s'] / P.SEC_PER_HOUR,
                         'delta_h': (t_end - runs['q3_baseline']['t_end_s']) / P.SEC_PER_HOUR,
                         'delta_pct': 100.0 * (t_end - runs['q3_baseline']['t_end_s'])
                         / runs['q3_baseline']['t_end_s'], 'threshold': P.C_THR}

    # ---- 五个测点的逐点达标时刻（斜率图）：用**物质坐标** eta 而非固定物理半径。
    # 收缩体内固定 r 会在半径缩过它之后不再对应任何骨架物质点，两种口径就不可比；
    # 固定 eta 始终是同一物质点，且在问题三（R 恒为 2 cm）下正好落在 0/0.5/1/1.5/2 cm。
    eta_probe = [0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0]

    def probe_cross(s, t_stop):
        """在密扫网格上找该物质点含水率首次下穿阈值的时刻，线性定位到相邻两点之间。"""
        tt = np.linspace(0.0, t_stop, 4000)
        out = []
        for e in eta_probe:
            j = min(int(round(e * (N - 1))), N - 1)
            vals = np.array([float(s.sol(float(t))[N:][j]) for t in tt])
            below = np.nonzero(vals <= P.C_THR)[0]
            if not below.size:
                out.append(None)
                continue
            i2 = int(below[0])
            if i2 == 0:
                out.append(0.0)
                continue
            v1, v2, t1, t2 = vals[i2 - 1], vals[i2], tt[i2 - 1], tt[i2]
            tc = t2 if v2 == v1 else t1 + (t2 - t1) * (v1 - P.C_THR) / (v1 - v2)
            out.append(float(tc / P.SEC_PER_HOUR))
        return out

    res['compare_q3'].update({
        'probe_eta': eta_probe,
        'probe_r_cm_q3': [round(e * P.R0_CM, 4) for e in eta_probe],
        'probe_r_cm_q4_at_t_end': [round(e * U.R_of_t(t_end) * P.CM_PER_M, 4) for e in eta_probe],
        'probe_cross_h_q4': probe_cross(sol, t_end),
        'probe_cross_h_q3': probe_cross(sols['q3_baseline'], runs['q3_baseline']['t_end_s']),
        'attribution_runs_h': {k: runs[k]['t_end_s'] / P.SEC_PER_HOUR
                               for k in ('q3_baseline', 'app4_noshrink', 'app3_shrink',
                                         'app4_shrink')},
        'probe_note': '测点取物质坐标 eta=r/R(t)；问题三 R 恒为 2 cm，故 eta 与固定物理半径一一对应。'
                      '归因三次运行（R1 附录4+固定域 / R2 附录3+收缩 / R3 附录4+收缩）一并给出，'
                      '供斜率图叠加'})
    st = slice(None, None, 10)
    res['mass_balance'] = {'t_s': tb[st].tolist(), 't_h': (tb[st] / P.SEC_PER_HOUR).tolist(),
                          'm_w_kg_per_m': np.round(mw_b[st], 8).tolist(),
                          'cumulative_loss_kg_per_m': np.round(cum[st], 8).tolist(),
                          'flux_rate_kg_per_m_s': [float(x) for x in rate[st]],
                          'stored_drop_kg_per_m': float(drop),
                          'closure_rel_gap': float(closure)}
    res['drying_curve'] = {'t_s': times[st], 't_h': [t / P.SEC_PER_HOUR for t in times[st]],
                           'C_max': np.round(cmax_ser[st], 6).tolist(),
                           'C_volavg': np.round(vavg_ser[st], 6).tolist(),
                           'C_center': jnan(np.round(Cg[st, 0], 6)),
                           'C_surface': np.round(Cs_ser[st], 6).tolist(),
                           'T_center': jnan(np.round(Tg[st, 0], 6)),
                           'T_surface': np.round(Ts_ser[st], 6).tolist(),
                           'R_cm': np.round(R_ser[st], 6).tolist(), 'threshold': P.C_THR}
    sh = slice(None, None, 20)
    res['heatmap'] = {'t_s': times[sh], 't_h': [t / P.SEC_PER_HOUR for t in times[sh]],
                      'r_cm': cols[:-1], 'surface_label': P.SURFACE_LABEL,
                      'R_cm': np.round(R_ser[sh], 6).tolist(),
                      'C': [jnan(r) for r in np.round(Cg[sh, :-1], 4)],
                      'T': [jnan(r) for r in np.round(Tg[sh, :-1], 4)],
                      'C_surface': np.round(Cs_ser[sh], 4).tolist(),
                      'T_surface': np.round(Ts_ser[sh], 4).tolist()}
    res['summary'] = {'t_end_h': t_end / P.SEC_PER_HOUR, 't_end_days': t_end / (24 * P.SEC_PER_HOUR),
                      'R_min_cm': float(R_ser.min()), 'R_max_cm': float(R_ser.max()),
                      'R_at_t_end_cm': U.R_of_t(t_end) * P.CM_PER_M,
                      'C_max_at_t_end': float(C_e.max()), 'C_center_at_t_end': float(C_e[0]),
                      'C_surface_at_t_end': float(U.surface_pair(cfg, y_e[:N], C_e, t_end,
                                                                R_m=U.R_of_t(t_end))[1]),
                      'volavg_C_at_t_end': float(U.vol_average(C_e, cfg['V_cell'])),
                      'T_center_at_t_end': float(y_e[0]),
                      'herb_T_max': float(np.nanmax(Tg)), 'herb_T_min': float(np.nanmin(Tg)),
                      'herb_C_max': float(np.nanmax(Cg)), 'herb_C_min': float(np.nanmin(Cg)),
                      'm_w_initial_kg_per_m': float(mw_b[0]), 'm_w_final_kg_per_m': float(mw_b[-1]),
                      'water_removed_pct': float(100.0 * drop / mw_b[0]),
                      'rho_d0': U.RHO_D0, 'rho_d_at_t_end': U.rho_d_of_t(t_end),
                      'n_output_rows': len(times), 'n_blank_cells': rt['Sheet1']['blank_cells'],
                      'D_at_center_t_end_m2_s': float(U.props_appendix_4(
                          np.array([C_e[0]]), np.array([y_e[0]]))[3][0]),
                      'D_at_t0_m2_s': float(U.props_appendix_4(
                          np.array([P.C0]), np.array([P.T0_C]))[3][0])}
    res['xlsx_report'] = rt
    res['checks'] = ck
    res['all_checks_passed'] = bool(all(v.get('passed') for v in ck.values()))
    return res, ck, sol, cfg, t_end, times, Cg, Tg


# ------------------------------------------------------------ 描述性参考（不参与求解）
def _weibull_R(t, R_inf, tau, k):
    return R_inf + (P.R0_CM - R_inf) * np.exp(-(np.asarray(t, float) / tau) ** k)


def radius_weibull_reference(t):
    """附件 2 收缩轨迹的 Weibull 参考拟合，只用于作图与报告收缩标度。

    本函数不参与任何 PDE 求解：问题四方程里的 R(t) 一律取附件 2 的保单调 PCHIP 插值
    （U.R_of_t），拟合式仅回答『实测收缩曲线像不像 Weibull』这一描述性问题。
    """
    if getattr(radius_weibull_reference, 'params', None) is None:
        from scipy.optimize import curve_fit
        p, _ = curve_fit(_weibull_R, U.R_T_GRID, U.R_DATA_CM, p0=[1.15, 5.0e4, 1.0],
                         bounds=([0.5, 1.0e3, 0.3], [2.0, 1.0e6, 3.0]), maxfev=20000)
        pred = _weibull_R(U.R_T_GRID, *p)
        radius_weibull_reference.params = p
        radius_weibull_reference.rmse_cm = float(np.sqrt(np.mean((pred - U.R_DATA_CM) ** 2)))
        radius_weibull_reference.max_abs_cm = float(np.abs(pred - U.R_DATA_CM).max())
    return _weibull_R(t, *radius_weibull_reference.params)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    out = run()[0]
    P.FIG_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    (P.FIG_DIR / 'problem_4_results.json').write_text(
        json.dumps(out, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding='utf-8')
    for k, v in out['checks'].items():
        print(f"{k}: {'PASS' if v.get('passed') else 'FAIL'}  {v.get('name', '')}")
    print('Q4 checks passed:', out['all_checks_passed'])
    print('t_end = %.3f s = %.4f h = %.4f d (R = %.4f cm)'
          % (out['t_end']['seconds'], out['t_end']['hours'], out['t_end']['days'],
             out['t_end']['R_at_t_end_cm']))
    a = out['attribution']
    for k in ('q3_baseline', 'app4_noshrink', 'app3_shrink', 'app4_shrink'):
        print('  %-14s t_end = %8.4f h' % (k, a[k]['t_end_h']))
    s = out['summary']
    print('water removed %.2f%% (%.4f -> %.4f kg/m); blanks %d'
          % (s['water_removed_pct'], s['m_w_initial_kg_per_m'], s['m_w_final_kg_per_m'],
             s['n_blank_cells']))
```

**B.8　sensitivity_analysis.py**

```python
# code/sensitivity_analysis.py — 第 3 层：单因子敏感性、二维响应面、LHS 全局不确定度
import json
import time

import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))   # 本机为嵌入式发行版，不自动加脚本目录

import params as P
import utils as U

BASE_APPENDIX = 'appendix_3'          # 以问题三（附录 3 固定域）为敏感性基准
Q4_APPENDIX = 'appendix_4'            # 问题四口径（附录 4 + 径向收缩），用于同源配对的鲁棒性对比
SENS_FACTORS = (
    ('h_m', 'h_m', 0.30, '对流传质系数 h_m'),
    ('h', 'h', 0.30, '对流换热系数 h'),
    ('ta_plateau', 'abs', 1.0, '平台热风温度 Ta'),
    ('ca_plateau', 'ca_plateau', 0.20, '平台空气含湿量 Ca'),
    ('d_scale', 'd_scale', 0.20, '扩散前因子 D0'),
    ('act_scale', 'act', 0.05, 'Arrhenius 活化温度 3850 K'),
    ('C0', 'C0', 0.05, '初始含水率 C0'),
    ('moist_scale', 'moist_scale', 0.10, '水分抑制常数 b'),
)
LATENT_SCAN = (0.0, 0.10, 0.25, 0.50)


def build_kwargs(factor, mult):
    """把『因子名 + 相对/绝对扰动』翻译成 make_config 的关键字与初值缩放。"""
    kw, c0_scale = {}, 1.0
    if factor == 'h_m':
        kw['h_m'] = P.HM_MASS * mult
    elif factor == 'h':
        kw['h'] = P.H_CONV * mult
    elif factor == 'ta_plateau':
        kw['ta_plateau'] = U.TA_PLATEAU + (mult - 1.0)
    elif factor == 'ca_plateau':
        kw['ca_plateau'] = U.CA_PLATEAU * mult
    elif factor == 'd_scale':
        kw['d_scale'] = mult
    elif factor == 'act_scale':
        kw['act_scale'] = mult
    elif factor == 'moist_scale':
        kw['moist_scale'] = mult
    elif factor == 'C0':
        c0_scale = mult
    elif factor == 'chi':
        kw['chi'] = mult
    return kw, c0_scale


def t_end_at(factor='none', mult=1.0, extra=None, N=P.N_CELLS):
    """单点真实重算：每次都独立积分到事件触发，绝不做插值或缩放外推。"""
    kw, c0_scale = build_kwargs(factor, mult)
    if extra:
        for f2, m2 in extra.items():
            k2, s2 = build_kwargs(f2, m2)
            kw.update(k2)
            c0_scale *= s2
    cfg = U.make_config(BASE_APPENDIX, N=N, **kw)
    y0 = np.concatenate((np.full(N, P.T0_C), np.full(N, P.C0 * c0_scale)))
    sol = U.integrate(lambda t, y: U.fixed_domain_rhs(t, y, cfg), (0.0, P.T_INTEGRATION_CAP), y0,
                      N=N, events=U.event_moisture_threshold, max_step=P.MAX_STEP_LONG)
    assert sol.success, sol.message
    if not len(sol.t_events[0]):
        return float('nan'), int(sol.nfev)
    return float(sol.t_events[0][0]), int(sol.nfev)


def solve_ivp_to_event(cfg, y0):
    """④M6 合同的显式落地：BDF + rtol/atol 定值 + 稀疏雅可比 + 阈值事件终止。"""
    import scipy.integrate as si
    return si.solve_ivp(lambda t, y: U.fixed_domain_rhs(t, y, cfg),
                        (0.0, P.T_INTEGRATION_CAP), y0, method='BDF', rtol=P.RTOL, atol=P.ATOL,
                        jac_sparsity=U.jacobian_sparsity(cfg['N']),
                        events=U.event_moisture_threshold, max_step=P.MAX_STEP_LONG)


def one_factor_scan(base_t):
    """单因子双向扰动（OAT）：每个方向都真实重算一次，不做任何线性外推。"""
    rows = []
    for name, kind, amp, label in SENS_FACTORS:
        for sign in (-1, +1):
```
