            mult = (1.0 + sign * amp) if kind != 'abs' else (1.0 + sign * amp)
            cfg_kw, c0_scale = build_kwargs(name, mult)
            cfg = U.make_config(BASE_APPENDIX, **cfg_kw)
            y0 = np.concatenate((np.full(cfg['N'], P.T0_C), np.full(cfg['N'], P.C0 * c0_scale)))
            sol = solve_ivp_to_event(cfg, y0)
            assert sol.success and len(sol.t_events[0]) > 0, name
            t_e = float(sol.t_events[0][0])
            rows.append({'factor': name, 'label': label, 'kind': kind, 'sign': sign,
                         'amplitude': amp, 'multiplier': mult, 't_end_s': t_e,
                         't_end_h': t_e / P.SEC_PER_HOUR, 'nfev': int(sol.nfev),
                         'delta_h': (t_e - base_t) / P.SEC_PER_HOUR,
                         'delta_pct': 100.0 * (t_e - base_t) / base_t})
    return rows


def latent_heat_scan(base_t):
    """潜热耦合强度 chi 的情景扫描：chi=0 为本文基准（题面未给潜热与相变数据）。"""
    rows = []
    for chi in LATENT_SCAN:
        cfg = U.make_config(BASE_APPENDIX, chi=chi)
        sol = solve_ivp_to_event(cfg, U.initial_state(cfg['N']))
        assert sol.success, sol.message
        fired = len(sol.t_events[0]) > 0
        t_e = float(sol.t_events[0][0]) if fired else None
        rows.append({'chi': chi, 'fired': bool(fired), 't_end_s': t_e,
                     't_end_h': (t_e / P.SEC_PER_HOUR) if fired else None,
                     'delta_pct': (100.0 * (t_e - base_t) / base_t) if fired else None,
                     'T_center_min_C': float(sol.y[0].min()),
                     'T_field_min_C': float(sol.y[:cfg['N']].min()),
                     'T_center_at_1800s_C': float(np.interp(1800.0, sol.t, sol.y[0])),
                     'nfev': int(sol.nfev)})
    return rows


def response_surface(n=7, amp_hm=0.30, amp_d=0.20):
    """h_m x D0 的 7x7 响应面：两个最强因子的交互，49 个点全部真实重算。"""
    hm = np.linspace(1.0 - amp_hm, 1.0 + amp_hm, n)
    d0 = np.linspace(1.0 - amp_d, 1.0 + amp_d, n)
    grid = np.full((n, n), np.nan)
    for i, a in enumerate(hm):
        for j, b in enumerate(d0):
            cfg = U.make_config(BASE_APPENDIX, h_m=P.HM_MASS * a, d_scale=b)
            sol = solve_ivp_to_event(cfg, U.initial_state(cfg['N']))
            assert sol.success and len(sol.t_events[0]) > 0, (a, b)
            grid[i, j] = float(sol.t_events[0][0]) / P.SEC_PER_HOUR
    return hm, d0, grid


def lhs_matrix(n, k, seed=P.SEED):
    """分层拉丁超立方：每因子各层只取一次，随机数发生器由 SEED 定死（可复现）。"""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    u = (rng.permuted(np.tile(np.arange(n, dtype=float), (k, 1)), axis=1) + rng.random((k, n))) / n
    return u.T


def lhs_study(n_lhs, base_t):
    """LHS 全局不确定度：7 因子同时扰动，每个样本点独立真实重算。"""
    facs = [f for f in SENS_FACTORS]
    u = lhs_matrix(n_lhs, len(facs))
    rows = []
    for p in range(n_lhs):
        kw, c0_scale, mults = {}, 1.0, {}
        for j, (name, kind, amp, _lab) in enumerate(facs):
            mult = 1.0 - amp + 2.0 * amp * float(u[p, j])
            mults[name] = mult
            k2, s2 = build_kwargs(name, mult)
            kw.update(k2)
            c0_scale *= s2
        cfg = U.make_config(BASE_APPENDIX, **kw)
        y0 = np.concatenate((np.full(cfg['N'], P.T0_C), np.full(cfg['N'], P.C0 * c0_scale)))
        sol = solve_ivp_to_event(cfg, y0)
        assert sol.success and len(sol.t_events[0]) > 0, 'LHS 点 %d 未触发事件' % p
        t_e = float(sol.t_events[0][0])
        rows.append({'i': p, 'multipliers': mults, 't_end_s': t_e,
                     't_end_h': t_e / P.SEC_PER_HOUR, 'nfev': int(sol.nfev),
                     'delta_pct': 100.0 * (t_e - base_t) / base_t})
    return rows


def lhs_study_q4(n_lhs, base_t_q4):
    """问题四口径（附录 4 + 径向收缩）的 LHS：复用与问题三**完全相同**的 LHS 矩阵，
    使第 i 个样本在两种口径下的因子取值逐位一致，从而可做配对对比而非两套独立随机。"""
    facs = [f for f in SENS_FACTORS]
    u = lhs_matrix(n_lhs, len(facs))
    rows = []
    for p in range(n_lhs):
        kw, c0_scale, mults = {}, 1.0, {}
        for j, (name, kind, amp, _lab) in enumerate(facs):
            mult = 1.0 - amp + 2.0 * amp * float(u[p, j])
            mults[name] = mult
            k2, s2 = build_kwargs(name, mult)
            kw.update(k2)
            c0_scale *= s2
        cfg = U.make_config(Q4_APPENDIX, **kw)
        y0 = np.concatenate((np.full(cfg['N'], P.T0_C), np.full(cfg['N'], P.C0 * c0_scale)))
        sol = U.integrate(lambda t, y: U.moving_domain_rhs(t, y, cfg),
                          (0.0, P.T_INTEGRATION_CAP), y0, N=cfg['N'],
                          events=U.event_moisture_threshold, max_step=P.MAX_STEP_LONG)
        assert sol.success and len(sol.t_events[0]) > 0, '问题四 LHS 点 %d 未触发事件' % p
        t_e = float(sol.t_events[0][0])
        rows.append({'i': p, 'multipliers': mults, 't_end_s': t_e,
                     't_end_h': t_e / P.SEC_PER_HOUR, 'nfev': int(sol.nfev),
                     'delta_pct': 100.0 * (t_e - base_t_q4) / base_t_q4})
    return rows


def t_end_q4_baseline():
    """问题四口径的未扰动基准，供上面的 delta_pct 归一。"""
    cfg = U.make_config(Q4_APPENDIX)
    sol = U.integrate(lambda t, y: U.moving_domain_rhs(t, y, cfg),
                      (0.0, P.T_INTEGRATION_CAP), U.initial_state(cfg['N']), N=cfg['N'],
                      events=U.event_moisture_threshold, max_step=P.MAX_STEP_LONG)
    assert sol.success and len(sol.t_events[0]) > 0
    return float(sol.t_events[0][0]), int(sol.nfev)


def spearman(x, y):
    """秩相关系数：LHS 样本上的全局敏感性指标（不假设线性）。"""
    rx = np.argsort(np.argsort(np.asarray(x, float))).astype(float)
    ry = np.argsort(np.argsort(np.asarray(y, float))).astype(float)
    rx -= rx.mean()
    ry -= ry.mean()
    return float(rx @ ry / np.sqrt((rx @ rx) * (ry @ ry)))


def _cached(key, parameters, validate, compute):
    """按 §14 要求给每个昂贵算例加原子检查点；依赖变动会自动失效。"""
    import sys
    from pathlib import Path
    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
    sys.path.insert(0, str(root / '_utils'))
    from compute_checkpoint import CaseCheckpoint
    deps = [Path(__file__).resolve(), root / 'code' / 'utils.py', root / 'code' / 'params.py',
            P.ATT1_XLSX, P.ATT2_XLSX]
    cp = CaseCheckpoint(root, key, dependencies=deps, parameters=parameters)
    data = cp.load(validate)
    if data is not None:
        return data, True
    data = compute()
    cp.save(data, validate)
    return data, False


def run(n_lhs=200):
    t0 = time.time()
    res = {'base_appendix': BASE_APPENDIX, 'seed': P.SEED, 'n_lhs': n_lhs,
           'factors': [{'name': n, 'kind': k, 'amplitude': a, 'label': l}
                       for n, k, a, l in SENS_FACTORS]}
    ck = {}
    base_t, base_nfev = t_end_at()
    res['baseline'] = {'t_end_s': base_t, 't_end_h': base_t / P.SEC_PER_HOUR, 'nfev': base_nfev}

    ok_oat = (lambda d: isinstance(d, dict) and len(d['oat']) == 2 * len(SENS_FACTORS)
              and len(d['latent']) == len(LATENT_SCAN)
              and all(r['nfev'] > 0 and r['t_end_s'] > 0 for r in d['oat']))
    oat_pack, hit_oat = _cached('sens_oat_latent',
                                {'factors': [list(f[:3]) for f in SENS_FACTORS],
                                 'latent': list(LATENT_SCAN), 'N': P.N_CELLS},
                                ok_oat, lambda: {'oat': one_factor_scan(base_t),
                                                 'latent': latent_heat_scan(base_t)})
    oat, latent = oat_pack['oat'], oat_pack['latent']

    ok_srf = (lambda d: isinstance(d, dict) and len(d['grid']) == 7 and len(d['grid'][0]) == 7
              and all(np.isfinite(v) and v > 0 for row in d['grid'] for v in row))
    srf, hit_srf = _cached('sens_surface_hm_d0', {'n': 7, 'amp_hm': 0.30, 'amp_d': 0.20},
                           ok_srf, lambda: (lambda hm, d0, g: {
                               'hm_mult': hm.tolist(), 'd0_mult': d0.tolist(),
                               'grid': np.round(g, 6).tolist()})(*response_surface()))

    ok_lhs = (lambda d: isinstance(d, list) and len(d) == n_lhs
              and all(r['nfev'] > 0 and r['t_end_s'] > 0 for r in d)
              and len({round(r['t_end_s'], 6) for r in d}) == n_lhs)
    lhs, hit_lhs = _cached('sens_lhs_%d' % n_lhs,
                           {'n': n_lhs, 'seed': P.SEED,
                            'factors': [list(f[:3]) for f in SENS_FACTORS]},
                           ok_lhs, lambda: lhs_study(n_lhs, base_t))

    base_t4, base_nfev4 = t_end_q4_baseline()
    res['baseline_q4'] = {'t_end_s': base_t4, 't_end_h': base_t4 / P.SEC_PER_HOUR,
                          'nfev': base_nfev4, 'appendix': Q4_APPENDIX, 'moving_boundary': True}

    ok_lhs4 = (lambda d: isinstance(d, list) and len(d) == n_lhs
               and all(r['nfev'] > 0 and r['t_end_s'] > 0 for r in d)
               and len({round(r['t_end_s'], 6) for r in d}) == n_lhs)
    lhs4, hit_lhs4 = _cached('sens_lhs_q4_%d' % n_lhs,
                             {'n': n_lhs, 'seed': P.SEED, 'appendix': Q4_APPENDIX, 'moving': True,
                              'factors': [list(f[:3]) for f in SENS_FACTORS]},
                             ok_lhs4, lambda: lhs_study_q4(n_lhs, base_t4))

    # ---- C23 真实重算核验：每个 OAT 点都必须与基准显著不同，LHS 样本互不相同
    gaps = [abs(r['t_end_s'] - base_t) for r in oat]
    ts = np.array([r['t_end_s'] for r in lhs])
    ts4 = np.array([r['t_end_s'] for r in lhs4])
    pair_min = float(np.min(np.diff(np.sort(ts))))
    pair_min4 = float(np.min(np.diff(np.sort(ts4))))
    ck['C23'] = {'name': '敏感性逐点真实重算', 'n_oat_points': len(oat), 'n_lhs_points': len(lhs),
                 'n_lhs_q4_points': len(lhs4),
                 'n_surface_points': 49, 'min_abs_gap_to_baseline_s': float(min(gaps)),
                 'tol_gap_s': 60.0, 'lhs_min_pairwise_gap_s': pair_min,
                 'lhs_q4_min_pairwise_gap_s': pair_min4,
                 'n_distinct_lhs_t_end': int(len({round(v, 6) for v in ts})),
                 'n_distinct_lhs_q4_t_end': int(len({round(v, 6) for v in ts4})),
                 'total_real_solves': len(oat) + len(latent) + 49 + len(lhs) + len(lhs4) + 2,
                 'checkpoint_hits': {'oat_latent': hit_oat, 'surface': hit_srf, 'lhs': hit_lhs,
                                     'lhs_q4': hit_lhs4},
                 'passed': bool(min(gaps) > 60.0 and pair_min > 0.0 and pair_min4 > 0.0
                                and len({round(v, 6) for v in ts}) == len(lhs)
                                and len({round(v, 6) for v in ts4}) == len(lhs4))}

    # ---- 单因子排序（龙卷风图）与全局秩相关
    by_factor = {}
    for n, k, a, l in SENS_FACTORS:
        lo = next(r for r in oat if r['factor'] == n and r['sign'] < 0)
        hi = next(r for r in oat if r['factor'] == n and r['sign'] > 0)
        by_factor[n] = {'label': l, 'amplitude': a, 'kind': k,
                        't_end_h_low': lo['t_end_h'], 't_end_h_high': hi['t_end_h'],
                        'delta_pct_low': lo['delta_pct'], 'delta_pct_high': hi['delta_pct'],
                        'half_range_h': 0.5 * abs(hi['t_end_h'] - lo['t_end_h']),
                        'elasticity': ((hi['t_end_s'] - lo['t_end_s']) / base_t)
                        / (hi['multiplier'] - lo['multiplier'])}
    order = sorted(by_factor, key=lambda k2: -by_factor[k2]['half_range_h'])
    spear = {n: spearman([r['multipliers'][n] for r in lhs], ts) for n, _k, _a, _l in SENS_FACTORS}
    ck['V3.9'] = {'name': '敏感性排序一致（OAT 首因子 = 秩相关首因子）',
                  'oat_rank': order, 'top_oat': order[0],
                  'top_spearman': max(spear, key=lambda k2: abs(spear[k2])),
                  'spearman': spear,
                  'passed': bool(order[0] == max(spear, key=lambda k2: abs(spear[k2])))}

    # ---- 物理方向性：七个因子的弹性符号必须与机理预期一致
    want = {'h_m': -1, 'h': -1, 'ta_plateau': -1, 'ca_plateau': +1, 'd_scale': -1,
            'act_scale': +1, 'C0': +1, 'moist_scale': +1}
    signs = {k: int(np.sign(v['elasticity'])) for k, v in by_factor.items()}
    ck['V3.10'] = {'name': '单因子弹性符号与机理一致', 'expected': want, 'observed': signs,
                   'mismatch': [k for k in want if signs[k] != want[k]],
                   'note': 'h_m/h/Ta/D0 增大加快干燥（t_end 下降），Ca/活化温度/C0/水分抑制常数 b '
                           '增大延长干燥（b 增大使 exp(-b/C) 在低含水率端急剧变小）',
                   'passed': bool(all(signs[k] == want[k] for k in want))}

    # ---- 响应面单调性：t_end 对 h_m、对 D0 都应逐行/逐列单调下降
    g = np.array(srf['grid'], float)
    row_mono = bool(np.all(np.diff(g, axis=1) < 0))
    col_mono = bool(np.all(np.diff(g, axis=0) < 0))
    ck['V3.11'] = {'name': '响应面双向单调', 'monotone_along_D0': row_mono,
                   'monotone_along_hm': col_mono, 'span_h': [float(g.min()), float(g.max())],
                   'corner_ratio': float(g.max() / g.min()), 'passed': bool(row_mono and col_mono)}

    # ---- ④M9 方向探针：t_end 必须随潜热折减系数 chi 单调增大（计入潜热 -> 降温 -> D 减小）
    lat_ok = [r for r in latent if r['fired']]
    seq = [r['t_end_s'] for r in lat_ok]
    mono_chi = all(seq[i] < seq[i + 1] for i in range(len(seq) - 1))
    ck['V3.4'] = {'name': '潜热方向探针（t_end 随 chi 单调增大）', 'chi_values': list(LATENT_SCAN),
                  'n_fired': len(lat_ok), 'n_scanned': len(latent),
                  't_end_h_by_chi': [r['t_end_h'] for r in latent],
                  'T_field_min_C_by_chi': [r['T_field_min_C'] for r in latent],
                  'monotone_increasing': bool(mono_chi),
                  'note': 'chi=0 为基准；未触发的 chi 说明其 t_end 超出保护上限 %g s，方向同样成立'
                          % P.T_INTEGRATION_CAP,
                  'passed': bool(mono_chi and (len(lat_ok) < len(latent) or seq[-1] > seq[0]))}

    d = ts / (24 * P.SEC_PER_HOUR)
    hist, edges = np.histogram(ts / P.SEC_PER_HOUR, bins=24)
    res['oat'] = oat
    res['tornado'] = {'order': order, 'by_factor': by_factor, 'baseline_h': base_t / P.SEC_PER_HOUR}
    res['latent_scan'] = {'chi_values': list(LATENT_SCAN), 'rows': latent,
                          'note': 'chi=0 为本文基准：题面未给潜热与相变速率数据，故不引入未给定参数；'
                                  'chi>0 只作情景界定'}
    res['response_surface'] = srf
    res['lhs'] = {'n': n_lhs, 'seed': P.SEED, 'rows': lhs,
                  't_end_h': {'mean': float(ts.mean() / P.SEC_PER_HOUR),
                              'std': float(ts.std(ddof=1) / P.SEC_PER_HOUR),
                              'cv': float(ts.std(ddof=1) / ts.mean()),
                              'p05': float(np.percentile(ts, 5) / P.SEC_PER_HOUR),
                              'p50': float(np.percentile(ts, 50) / P.SEC_PER_HOUR),
                              'p95': float(np.percentile(ts, 95) / P.SEC_PER_HOUR),
                              'min': float(ts.min() / P.SEC_PER_HOUR),
                              'max': float(ts.max() / P.SEC_PER_HOUR)},
                  'fraction_in_2_3_days': float(np.mean((d >= P.DUR_DAYS_LOW)
                                                        & (d <= P.DUR_DAYS_HIGH))),
                  'fraction_below_2_days': float(np.mean(d < P.DUR_DAYS_LOW)),
                  'fraction_above_3_days': float(np.mean(d > P.DUR_DAYS_HIGH)),
                  'spearman': spear,
                  'histogram': {'counts': hist.tolist(), 'edges_h': edges.tolist()}}
    # ---- 问题三 / 问题四 配对鲁棒性：同一 LHS 矩阵下两种口径的 t_end 分布
    d4 = ts4 / (24 * P.SEC_PER_HOUR)
    hist4, edges4 = np.histogram(ts4 / P.SEC_PER_HOUR, bins=24)
    ratio = ts4 / ts
    paired = all(abs(a['multipliers'][n] - b['multipliers'][n]) < 1e-12
                 for a, b in zip(lhs, lhs4) for n, _k, _a, _l in SENS_FACTORS)
    ck['V3.13'] = {'name': '问题三/四鲁棒性配对同源（同一 LHS 矩阵）',
                   'n_pairs': len(lhs4), 'multipliers_identical': bool(paired),
                   'q4_shorter_fraction': float(np.mean(ts4 < ts)),
                   'ratio_q4_over_q3': {'mean': float(ratio.mean()), 'min': float(ratio.min()),
                                        'max': float(ratio.max())},
                   'note': '两条分布共用同一组因子取值，故雨云图是配对对比；'
                           'q4/q3 比值恒小于 1 即收缩缩短工期这一结论在全分布上稳健',
                   'passed': bool(paired and len(lhs4) == len(lhs))}

    res['lhs_q4'] = {'n': n_lhs, 'seed': P.SEED, 'appendix': Q4_APPENDIX, 'moving_boundary': True,
                     'rows': lhs4,
                     't_end_h': {'mean': float(ts4.mean() / P.SEC_PER_HOUR),
                                 'std': float(ts4.std(ddof=1) / P.SEC_PER_HOUR),
                                 'cv': float(ts4.std(ddof=1) / ts4.mean()),
                                 'p05': float(np.percentile(ts4, 5) / P.SEC_PER_HOUR),
                                 'p50': float(np.percentile(ts4, 50) / P.SEC_PER_HOUR),
                                 'p95': float(np.percentile(ts4, 95) / P.SEC_PER_HOUR),
                                 'min': float(ts4.min() / P.SEC_PER_HOUR),
                                 'max': float(ts4.max() / P.SEC_PER_HOUR)},
                     'fraction_in_2_3_days': float(np.mean((d4 >= P.DUR_DAYS_LOW)
                                                           & (d4 <= P.DUR_DAYS_HIGH))),
                     'fraction_below_2_days': float(np.mean(d4 < P.DUR_DAYS_LOW)),
                     'fraction_above_3_days': float(np.mean(d4 > P.DUR_DAYS_HIGH)),
                     'spearman': {n: spearman([r['multipliers'][n] for r in lhs4], ts4)
                                  for n, _k, _a, _l in SENS_FACTORS},
                     'histogram': {'counts': hist4.tolist(), 'edges_h': edges4.tolist()}}
    res['robustness_pair'] = {
        'label_q3': '问题三口径（附录 3 + 固定域）', 'label_q4': '问题四口径（附录 4 + 径向收缩）',
        'n': n_lhs, 'baseline_q3_h': base_t / P.SEC_PER_HOUR,
        'baseline_q4_h': base_t4 / P.SEC_PER_HOUR,
        't_end_h_q3': (ts / P.SEC_PER_HOUR).tolist(), 't_end_h_q4': (ts4 / P.SEC_PER_HOUR).tolist(),
        'ratio_q4_over_q3': ratio.tolist(),
        'q4_shorter_fraction': float(np.mean(ts4 < ts)),
        'note': '同一 LHS 矩阵驱动两种口径，逐样本配对；雨云图左右两半即这两列'}

    res['checks'] = ck
    res['all_checks_passed'] = bool(all(v.get('passed') for v in ck.values()))
    res['wall_time_s'] = time.time() - t0
    return res, ck


if __name__ == '__main__':
    out = run()[0]
    P.FIG_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    (P.FIG_DIR / 'sensitivity_results.json').write_text(
        json.dumps(out, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding='utf-8')
    for k, v in out['checks'].items():
        print(f"{k}: {'PASS' if v.get('passed') else 'FAIL'}  {v.get('name', '')}")
    print('SENS checks passed:', out['all_checks_passed'])
    print('baseline t_end = %.4f h' % out['baseline']['t_end_h'])
    print('OAT order:', ' > '.join(out['tornado']['order']))
    for k in out['tornado']['order']:
        b = out['tornado']['by_factor'][k]
        print('  %-12s +-%-5g  t_end %8.4f .. %8.4f h  (%+.2f%% .. %+.2f%%)'
              % (k, b['amplitude'], b['t_end_h_low'], b['t_end_h_high'],
                 b['delta_pct_low'], b['delta_pct_high']))
    s = out['lhs']['t_end_h']
    print('LHS n=%d: p05/p50/p95 = %.3f / %.3f / %.3f h, CV = %.4f'
          % (out['lhs']['n'], s['p05'], s['p50'], s['p95'], out['lhs']['t_end_h']['cv']))
    print('fraction in 2-3 d: %.3f | wall %.1f s' % (out['lhs']['fraction_in_2_3_days'],
                                                     out['wall_time_s']))
```

**B.9　sanity_check.py**

                                 'problem_2_results.heatmap.T', 'problem_2_results.heatmap.C'],
    'fig_q2_stage_compare': ['problem_2_results.table_3_temperature.values',

# 关键标量的物理区间：越界即为硬失败
RANGES = {
    ('problem_1_results', 'T_center_1800s'): (P.T0_C, 41.5130),
    ('problem_1_results', 'T_surface_1800s'): (P.T0_C, 41.5130),
    ('problem_1_results', 'C_center_1800s'): (P.C_THR, P.C0),
    ('problem_1_results', 'C_surface_1800s'): (0.0, P.C0),
    ('problem_1_results', 'herb_T_max'): (P.T0_C, 41.5130),
    ('problem_1_results', 'herb_T_min'): (P.T0_C - 1e-9, 41.5130),
    ('problem_1_results', 'herb_C_max'): (0.0, P.C0),
    ('problem_2_results', 'T_center_end'): (P.T0_C, 50.2460),
    ('problem_2_results', 'T_surface_end'): (P.T0_C, 50.2460),
    ('problem_2_results', 'C_center_end'): (P.C_THR, P.C0),
    ('problem_2_results', 'volavg_C_end'): (0.0, P.C0),
    ('problem_3_results', 't_end_h'): (1.0, 240.0),
    ('problem_3_results', 't_end_days'): (0.1 * P.DUR_DAYS_LOW, 10 * P.DUR_DAYS_HIGH),
    ('problem_4_results', 't_end_h'): (1.0, 240.0),
    ('problem_4_results', 't_end_days'): (0.1 * P.DUR_DAYS_LOW, 10 * P.DUR_DAYS_HIGH),
    ('problem_4_results', 'R_min_cm'): (1.198, P.R0_CM),
    ('problem_4_results', 'R_max_cm'): (1.198, P.R0_CM),
}


def _walk_numbers(node, path='', out=None, cap=200000):
    """遍历 JSON 里的全部数值，用于统一做有限性与 NaN 体检。"""
    if out is None:
        out = []
    if len(out) >= cap:
        return out
    if isinstance(node, dict):
        for k, v in node.items():
            _walk_numbers(v, f'{path}.{k}', out, cap)
    elif isinstance(node, (list, tuple)):
        for i, v in enumerate(node):
            _walk_numbers(v, f'{path}[{i}]', out, cap)
    elif isinstance(node, bool):
        pass
    elif isinstance(node, (int, float)):
        out.append((path, float(node)))
    return out


def _same_label(got, want):
    """表头比对：pandas 会把 '2.0' 读成数值 2.0（打印为 2），故数值列按数值比。"""
    a, b = str(got).strip(), str(want).strip()
    if a == b:
        return True
    try:
        return abs(float(a) - float(b)) < 1e-12
    except ValueError:
        return False


def check_xlsx():
    """写盘文件体检：行列数、表头口径、空白单元格分布（只报数字，不打印内容）。"""
    import pandas as pd
    rows = []
    spec = {1: (len(P.Q1_OUT_TIMES), 22, ['温度', '水分浓度'], '2.0'),
            2: (len(P.Q2_OUT_TIMES), 22, ['温度', '水分浓度'], '2.0'),
            3: (None, 22, ['Sheet1'], '2.0'),
            4: (None, 22, ['Sheet1'], P.SURFACE_LABEL)}
    for k, (n_rows, n_cols, sheets, last_col) in spec.items():
        xl = pd.ExcelFile(P.RESULT_FILES[k])
        got_sheets = list(xl.sheet_names)
        for sh in got_sheets:
            df = xl.parse(sh, header=0)
            body = df.iloc[:, 1:]
            n_blank = int(body.isna().to_numpy().sum())
            rows.append({'file': f'result{k}.xlsx', 'sheet': sh, 'n_rows': int(len(df)),
                         'n_cols': int(df.shape[1]),
                         'last_col': str(df.columns[-1]).strip(),
                         'n_blank_cells': n_blank,
                         'rows_ok': (n_rows is None or len(df) == n_rows),
                         'cols_ok': df.shape[1] == n_cols,
                         'last_col_ok': _same_label(df.columns[-1], last_col),
                         'sheets_ok': got_sheets == sheets,
                         'blank_ok': (n_blank == 0 if k != 4 else n_blank > 0)})
    return rows


def check_tables():
    """表 1—表 4 的内容判据（P1-C3、P2-C3）：行时刻、列位置、四位小数、逐格与 result*.xlsx 相符。

    能力项的 required_output 是 paper/main.md，属后续写稿步骤；本函数只核内容判据，
    使写稿步骤嵌入 figures/TABLE_*.md 后即可直接闭合，不必重算 PDE。
    """
    import re

    import pandas as pd
    spec = ((1, '温度', 'TABLE_1', P.Q1_TABLE_TIMES, 1.0),
            (1, '水分浓度', 'TABLE_2', P.Q1_TABLE_TIMES, 1.0),
            (2, '温度', 'TABLE_3', P.Q2_TABLE_TIMES, P.SEC_PER_HOUR),
            (2, '水分浓度', 'TABLE_4', P.Q2_TABLE_TIMES, P.SEC_PER_HOUR))
    rows = []
    for k, sheet, tag, want_t, mul in spec:
        lines = [ln for ln in (P.FIG_DIR / f'{tag}.md').read_text(encoding='utf-8').splitlines()
                 if ln.startswith('|')]
        hdr = [c.strip() for c in lines[0].strip('|').split('|')]
        body = [[c.strip() for c in ln.strip('|').split('|')] for ln in lines[2:]]
        df = pd.read_excel(P.RESULT_FILES[k], sheet_name=sheet)
        col_of = {}
        for c in df.columns[1:]:
            try:
                col_of[round(float(c), 6)] = c
            except (TypeError, ValueError):
                pass
        got_t = [float(r[0]) * mul for r in body]
        max_d, n_cmp = 0.0, 0
        for r, ts in zip(body, want_t):
            sub = df[np.abs(df[df.columns[0]] - ts) < 1e-9]
            if len(sub) != 1:
                max_d = float('inf')
                break
            for rc, v in zip(P.TABLE_R_CM, r[1:]):
                max_d = max(max_d, abs(float(v) - round(float(sub.iloc[0][col_of[round(rc, 6)]]),
                                                        P.DECIMALS)))
                n_cmp += 1
        rows.append({'tag': tag, 'file': f'result{k}.xlsx', 'sheet': sheet, 'n_cells': n_cmp,
                     'max_abs_diff': max_d,
                     'cols_ok': hdr[1:] == [f'r = {c:g} cm' for c in P.TABLE_R_CM],
                     'times_ok': (len(got_t) == len(want_t)
                                  and all(abs(a - b) < 1e-9 for a, b in zip(got_t, want_t))),
                     'decimals_ok': all(re.fullmatch(r'-?\d+\.\d{%d}' % P.DECIMALS, v)
                                        for r in body for v in r[1:]),
                     'cells_ok': max_d < 5e-13})
    return rows


def run():
    fails, notes = [], []
    print('=== JSON 结构与出图键位 ===')
    loaded = {}
    for name, keys in FIGURE_KEYS.items():
        p = P.FIG_DIR / f'{name}.json'
        if not p.exists():
            fails.append(f'{name}: 文件缺失')
            print(f'{name}: MISSING')
            continue
        d = json.loads(p.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        loaded[name] = d
        missing = [k for k in keys if k not in d]
        size_kb = p.stat().st_size / 1024.0
        print(f'{name}: {"OK" if not missing else "MISSING " + ",".join(missing[:MAX_SAMPLES])}'
              f'  ({len(d)} keys, {size_kb:.0f} KB)')
        if missing:
            fails.append(f'{name}: 缺键 {missing[:MAX_SAMPLES]}')

    print('\n=== 数值有限性（NaN/Inf 体检）===')
    for name, d in loaded.items():
        nums = _walk_numbers(d, name)
        bad = [(p2, v) for p2, v in nums if not np.isfinite(v)]
        print(f'{name}: {len(nums)} 个数值，非有限 {len(bad)} 个'
              + ('' if not bad else '  样本 ' + '; '.join(f'{p2}={v}' for p2, v in bad[:MAX_SAMPLES])))
        if bad:
            fails.append(f'{name}: {len(bad)} 个非有限数值')

    print('\n=== 关键标量物理区间 ===')
    for (name, key), (lo, hi) in RANGES.items():
        d = loaded.get(name)
        if d is None:
            continue
        src = d.get('summary', {})
        val = src.get(key, d.get('t_end', {}).get(key.replace('t_end_', '')
                                                  if key.startswith('t_end_') else key))
        if val is None:
            fails.append(f'{name}.summary.{key} 缺失')
            print(f'{name}.{key}: MISSING')
            continue
        ok = lo - 1e-9 <= float(val) <= hi + 1e-9
        print(f'{name}.{key} = {float(val):.6g}  [{lo:g}, {hi:g}]  {"OK" if ok else "OUT OF RANGE"}')
        if not ok:
            fails.append(f'{name}.{key}={val} 越界 [{lo}, {hi}]')

    print('\n=== 全部自检项汇总 ===')
    n_ck = n_pass = 0
    for name, d in loaded.items():
        if name == 'all_results':
            continue
        ck = d.get('checks', {})
        bad = [k for k, v in ck.items() if not v.get('passed')]
        n_ck += len(ck)
        n_pass += len(ck) - len(bad)
        print(f'{name}: {len(ck) - len(bad)}/{len(ck)} passed'
              + ('' if not bad else '  FAILED: ' + ','.join(bad[:MAX_SAMPLES])))
        if bad:
            fails.append(f'{name}: 自检失败 {bad[:MAX_SAMPLES]}')
    print(f'合计 {n_pass}/{n_ck}')

    print('\n=== 竞赛结果文件 ===')
    for r in check_xlsx():
        flags = [k for k in ('rows_ok', 'cols_ok', 'last_col_ok', 'sheets_ok', 'blank_ok')
                 if not r[k]]
        print(f"{r['file']}[{r['sheet']}]: {r['n_rows']} 行 x {r['n_cols']} 列，末列 "
              f"{r['last_col']}，空白 {r['n_blank_cells']}  {'OK' if not flags else 'BAD ' + ','.join(flags)}")
        if flags:
            fails.append(f"{r['file']}[{r['sheet']}]: {flags}")

    print('\n=== FIGURE_MANIFEST 24 张 DATA 图的数据齐备性 ===')
    bad_fig = []
    for fig, paths in FIGURE_DATA_MAP.items():
        miss = []
        for dotted in paths:
            head, *rest = dotted.split('.')
            node = loaded.get(head)
            for seg in rest:
                node = node.get(seg) if isinstance(node, dict) else None
                if node is None:
                    break
            if node is None or (isinstance(node, (list, dict, str)) and len(node) == 0):
                miss.append(dotted)
        if miss:
            bad_fig.append((fig, miss))
    print(f'{len(FIGURE_DATA_MAP)} 张图，数据齐备 {len(FIGURE_DATA_MAP) - len(bad_fig)} 张')
    for fig, miss in bad_fig[:MAX_SAMPLES]:
        print(f'  MISSING {fig}: {miss[:3]}')
        fails.append(f'{fig} 缺数据 {miss[:3]}')

    print('\n=== 论文表 1—表 4 内容判据（P1-C3、P2-C3）===')
    for r in check_tables():
        flags = [k for k in ('cols_ok', 'times_ok', 'decimals_ok', 'cells_ok') if not r[k]]
        print(f"{r['tag']} vs {r['file']}[{r['sheet']}]: 逐格 {r['n_cells']} 点，"
              f"最大差 {r['max_abs_diff']:.3e}  {'OK' if not flags else 'BAD ' + ','.join(flags)}")
        if flags:
            fails.append(f"{r['tag']}: {flags}")

    print('\n=== 跨问题台账 ===')
    lp = P.ROOT / 'CROSS_PROBLEM_LEDGER.json'
    if lp.exists():
        led = json.loads(lp.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        n_obs = sum(1 for it in led['items'] if it.get('observed') is not None)
        print(f"{led['n_items']} 项，observed 已回填 {n_obs} 项，违规 {len(led['violations'])} 项"
              + ('' if not led['violations'] else '  ' + ','.join(led['violations'][:MAX_SAMPLES])))
        if led['violations']:
            fails.append(f"台账违规 {led['violations'][:MAX_SAMPLES]}")
        if n_obs != led['n_items']:
            fails.append('台账 observed 未全部回填')
        if not led['module_available']:
            notes.append('_utils/cross_problem_check.py 不存在（已如实记录模块名）')
    else:
        fails.append('CROSS_PROBLEM_LEDGER.json 缺失')

    print('\n=== 结论 ===')
    for n in notes:
        print('NOTE:', n)
    if fails:
        print('SANITY FAIL（%d 项）：' % len(fails))
        for f in fails[:MAX_SAMPLES]:
            print('  -', f)
    else:
        print('SANITY OK：结构、数值、区间、自检、写盘、台账全部通过')
    return fails


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(1 if run() else 0)
```

**B.10　constraint_audit.py**

```python
# code/constraint_audit.py — 约束复核：把 C01-C23 与 V 系列判据在写盘 JSON 上重新断言一次
# 与各问 run() 内的自检互为独立复核：这里只读 JSON，重算门限与序关系，不重跑 PDE。
import json

import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))   # 本机为嵌入式发行版，不自动加脚本目录

import params as P

MAX_SAMPLES = 5

# 契约表：约束号 -> (来源 JSON, checks 里的键, 该约束要复核的量与门限)
CONTRACT = [
    ('C01', 'problem_1_results', 'C01', '均匀场零通量', 'measured', '<', 1e-12),
    ('C02', 'problem_1_results', 'C02', '柱面通量正比于 r', 'measured', '<', 1e-12),
    ('C03', 'problem_1_results', 'C03', '双场独立（两场同时活跃且不共享内存）',
     'dC_absmax', '>', 0.0),
    ('C04', 'problem_1_results', 'C04', '表面值网格无关性', 'measured', '<', 1e-3),
    ('C05', 'problem_1_results', 'C05', 'Robin 闭合一致', 'measured_T', '<', 1e-10),
    ('C09', 'problem_1_results', 'C09', '半解析解 147 点对照', 'measured_max_abs_C', '<', 1e-3),
    ('C10', 'problem_1_results', 'C10', '问题一单向解耦', 'measured', '<', 1e-12),
    ('C12', 'problem_2_results', 'C12', '五个物性偏导非零', None, None, None),
    ('C08', 'problem_2_results', 'C08', '附件 1 复现与衔接连续', 'sample_rel_err_Ta', '<', 1e-9),
    ('C15', 'problem_2_results', 'C15', '模型对比两两可分辨', 'measured_min_gap', '>', 1e-6),
    ('C17', 'problem_3_results', 'C17', '判据口径序关系', 'gap_max_minus_volavg_s', '>', 60.0),
    ('C19', 'problem_3_results', 'C19', '首次下穿', 'measured_endpoint_gap', '<', 1e-5),
    ('C18', 'problem_4_results', 'C18', '半径单调且复现附件 2', 'sample_repro_max_abs_cm', '<', 1e-9),
    ('C20', 'problem_4_results', 'C20', '绝干骨架水量守恒', 'forward_rel_drift', '<', 1e-10),
    ('C21', 'problem_4_results', 'C21', '归因分解四例可分辨', 'min_pairwise_gap_s', '>', 60.0),
    ('C22', 'problem_4_results', 'C22', 't=0 收缩域退化为固定域', 'measured_max_abs_diff', '<', 1e-12),
    ('C23', 'sensitivity_results', 'C23', '敏感性逐点真实重算', 'min_abs_gap_to_baseline_s', '>', 60.0),
]


def load(name):
    return json.loads((P.FIG_DIR / f'{name}.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))


def run():
    J = {n: load(n) for n in ('problem_1_results', 'problem_2_results', 'problem_3_results',
                              'problem_4_results', 'sensitivity_results', 'all_results')}
    rows, fails = [], []

    def rec(cid, name, ok, detail):
        rows.append({'id': cid, 'name': name, 'passed': bool(ok), 'detail': detail})
        if not ok:
            fails.append(f'{cid} {name}: {detail}')

    # ---- 契约表逐条复核：既要 checks 自报 passed，也要门限在本文件里重算一遍
    for cid, src, key, name, field, op, tol in CONTRACT:
        ck = J[src]['checks'].get(key)
        if ck is None:
            rec(cid, name, False, f'{src}.checks.{key} 缺失')
            continue
        ok = bool(ck.get('passed'))
        det = f"self={ok}"
        if field is not None:
            v = ck.get(field)
            if v is None:
                ok, det = False, f'{field} 缺失'
            else:
                num = float(v)
                gate = (num < tol) if op == '<' else (num > tol)
                ok = ok and gate
                det = f'{field}={num:.6g} {op} {tol:g} -> {gate}'
        rec(cid, name, ok, det)

    # ---- C06/C07/C13/C14/C16/C11：在四问里重复出现，逐问复核
    for cid, name in (('C06', '剖面单调'), ('C07', '全域最大含水率在中心'),
                      ('C13', '干燥方向性'), ('C14', '低含水率下界保护'),
                      ('C16', '写盘-读回往返一致'), ('C11', '附录绑定')):
        hits = [(n, J[n]['checks'][cid]['passed']) for n in J
                if n != 'all_results' and cid in J[n].get('checks', {})]
        rec(cid, name + f'（{len(hits)} 处）', bool(hits) and all(p for _n, p in hits),
            ','.join(f'{n.split("_")[1]}:{int(p)}' for n, p in hits))

    # ---- 附录绑定的实际取值：问题一用附录 2，问题二三用附录 3，问题四用附录 4
    want_app = {'problem_1_results': 'appendix_2', 'problem_2_results': 'appendix_3',
                'problem_3_results': 'appendix_3', 'problem_4_results': 'appendix_4'}
    bad_app = [n for n, w in want_app.items() if J[n]['props_appendix'] != w]
    rec('C11b', '各问物性附录绑定正确', not bad_app,
        'ok' if not bad_app else f'错绑 {bad_app[:MAX_SAMPLES]}')

    # ---- C12 五个偏导都必须严格非零，三个惰性偏导必须严格为零
    p12 = J['problem_2_results']['checks']['C12']
    rec('C12b', '物性偏导结构（5 非零 / 3 恒零）',
        all(float(v) > 1e-12 for v in p12['partials'].values())
        and all(float(v) == 0.0 for v in p12['inert_partials_expected_zero'].values()),
        f"min|partial|={min(float(v) for v in p12['partials'].values()):.3e}")

    # ---- C20 反向对照：三个错误变体的漂移必须比正向大若干个数量级
    c20 = J['problem_4_results']['checks']['C20']
    fwd = float(c20['forward_rel_drift'])
    revs = {k: float(v) for k, v in c20['reverse_rel_drift'].items()}
    rec('C20b', 'C20 反向变体全部显著破坏守恒',
        all(v > 1e-4 for v in revs.values()) and fwd < 1e-10,
        f"forward={fwd:.3e}; reverse min={min(revs.values()):.4%}")

    # ---- C17 三判据序关系必须是 surface < volavg < max（误读『各处』会低估工期）
    cc = J['problem_3_results']['criterion_comparison']
    ts, tv, tm = (cc['surface']['t_end_h'], cc['volavg']['t_end_h'], cc['max']['t_end_h'])
    rec('C17b', '判据序关系 surface<volavg<max', ts < tv < tm,
        f'{ts:.4f} < {tv:.4f} < {tm:.4f} h')

    # ---- V3.6/C15 四级模型阶梯必须两两可分辨且方向合理
    ml = J['problem_2_results']['model_ladder']['paths']
    vals = {k: (ml[k]['volavg_C_end'] if 'volavg_C_end' in ml[k] else ml[k]['C_end'])
            for k in ml}
    gaps = [abs(a - b) for i, a in enumerate(vals.values())
            for b in list(vals.values())[i + 1:]]
    rec('V3.6', '四级模型阶梯两两可分辨', min(gaps) > 1e-6, f'min gap={min(gaps):.6g}')

    # ---- V3.10 敏感性弹性符号必须与机理一致
    v310 = J['sensitivity_results']['checks']['V3.10']
    rec('V3.10', '单因子弹性符号与机理一致', not v310['mismatch'],
        f"mismatch={v310['mismatch']}")

    # ---- V3.4 潜热方向探针：t_end 必须随 chi 单调增大
    v34 = J['sensitivity_results']['checks']['V3.4']
    seq = [x for x in v34['t_end_h_by_chi'] if x is not None]
    rec('V3.4', 't_end 随潜热系数 chi 单调增大',
        all(seq[i] < seq[i + 1] for i in range(len(seq) - 1)),
        ' < '.join(f'{x:.4f}' for x in seq) + ' h')

    # ---- V3.2 网格收敛：四问的最细两档差值都必须落在各自门限内
    g32 = []
    for n in ('problem_1_results', 'problem_2_results', 'problem_3_results', 'problem_4_results'):
        c = J[n]['checks'].get('V3.2')
        if c:
            g32.append((n.split('_')[1], bool(c['passed'])))
    rec('V3.2', '网格收敛阶梯（四问）', all(p for _n, p in g32),
        ','.join(f'{n}:{int(p)}' for n, p in g32))

    # ---- V3.3 时间容差：问题三、四的 t_end 变化都必须 < 60 s
    g33 = [(n.split('_')[1], float(J[n]['checks']['V3.3']['measured_dt_end_s']))
           for n in ('problem_3_results', 'problem_4_results')]
    rec('V3.3', '时间容差对照 t_end < 60 s', all(v < 60.0 for _n, v in g33),
        ','.join(f'{n}:{v:.3f}s' for n, v in g33))

    # ---- XA2 问题三前 10800 s 必须与问题二逐格恒等
    xa2 = float(J['problem_3_results']['checks']['XA2']['measured_max_abs_diff'])
    rec('XA2', '问题三与问题二逐格恒等', xa2 < 1e-9, f'{xa2:.3e}')

    # ---- V3.12 烘干时长与题面 2-3 天同量级
    d3 = J['problem_3_results']['summary']['t_end_days']
    d4 = J['problem_4_results']['summary']['t_end_days']
    rec('V3.12', '烘干时长与题面 2-3 天同量级',
        all(0.1 * P.DUR_DAYS_LOW <= d <= 10 * P.DUR_DAYS_HIGH for d in (d3, d4)),
        f'Q3={d3:.4f} d, Q4={d4:.4f} d（题面参照 {P.DUR_DAYS_LOW:g}-{P.DUR_DAYS_HIGH:g} d）')

    # ---- 单位与量纲：D 必须在 m^2/s 量级，Bi 必须 O(1)（否决集中参数）
    ch = J['problem_1_results']['characteristics']
    bi_ok = 0.1 < ch['Bi_T'] < 100 and 0.1 < ch['Bi_m'] < 100
    rec('U01', 'Biot 数 O(1)（集中参数不成立）', bi_ok,
        f"Bi_T={ch['Bi_T']:.4f}, Bi_m={ch['Bi_m']:.4f}")
    d_span = J['problem_2_results']['D_response_surface']
    rec('U02', 'D 落在 1e-11..1e-6 m^2/s',
        1e-11 < d_span['D_min'] and d_span['D_max'] < 1e-6,
        f"D in [{d_span['D_min']:.3e}, {d_span['D_max']:.3e}] m^2/s")

    # ---- 干基口径：含水率允许 > 1，且四问都不得出现归一化痕迹
    c_max = max(J[n]['summary'].get('herb_C_max', J[n]['summary'].get('C_max_at_t_end', 0.0))
                for n in ('problem_1_results', 'problem_2_results'))
    rec('U03', '干基含水率保留 >1 的量级（未被归一化）', c_max > 1.0,
        f'max C={c_max:.4f} kg/kg, C0={P.C0:g}')

    # ---- 台账：bound/observed/reason 三段式必须齐全且全部满足
    led = json.loads((P.ROOT / 'CROSS_PROBLEM_LEDGER.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    full = all(('bound' in it and it.get('observed') is not None and it.get('reason'))
               for it in led['items'])
    rec('XP', f"跨问题台账（{led['n_items']} 项）", full and led['all_satisfied'],
        f"observed 齐全={full}, 违规={led['violations']}")

    n_pass = sum(1 for r in rows if r['passed'])
    print('=== 约束复核（独立于各问自检，只读写盘 JSON）===')
    for r in rows:
        print(f"{r['id']:<7} {'PASS' if r['passed'] else 'FAIL'}  {r['name']}  |  {r['detail']}")
    print(f'\n约束复核 {n_pass}/{len(rows)} 通过')
    if fails:
        print('FAILED（至多列 %d 条）：' % MAX_SAMPLES)
        for f in fails[:MAX_SAMPLES]:
            print('  -', f)

    out = {'n_constraints': len(rows), 'n_passed': n_pass, 'all_passed': not fails,
           'rows': rows, 'failures': fails}
    (P.FIG_DIR / 'constraint_audit.json').write_text(
        json.dumps(out, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding='utf-8')
    return out


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(0 if run()['all_passed'] else 1)
```

**B.11　make_tables.py**

```python
# code/make_tables.py — 由写盘 JSON 生成 figures/TABLE_*.md，供写稿步骤直接嵌入（数据真实，杜绝编造）
import json

import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))   # 本机为嵌入式发行版，不自动加脚本目录

import params as P

BLANK = '—'          # 收缩后半径已小于该固定距离，物理上不存在该点


def _f(v, nd=P.DECIMALS):
    return BLANK if v is None else f'{float(v):.{nd}f}'


def _md(headers, rows, caption):
    out = [caption, '']
    out.append('| ' + ' | '.join(headers) + ' |')
    out.append('|' + '|'.join(['---'] * len(headers)) + '|')
    for r in rows:
        out.append('| ' + ' | '.join(r) + ' |')
    return '\n'.join(out) + '\n'


def load(n):
    return json.loads((P.FIG_DIR / f'{n}.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))


def run():
    r1, r2, r3, r4 = (load(f'problem_{i}_results') for i in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    made = []

    # ---- 表 1、表 2：问题一 7 时刻 x 5 位置
    for tag, key, unit, num in (('TABLE_1', 'table_1_temperature', '温度 / °C', 1),
                                ('TABLE_2', 'table_2_moisture', '含水率 / (kg/kg)', 2)):
        t = r1[key]
        hdr = ['时间 / s'] + [f'r = {r:g} cm' for r in t['radii_cm']]
        rows = [[f"{int(ts)}"] + [_f(v) for v in vals]
                for ts, vals in zip(t['times_s'], t['values'])]
        cap = (f'**表 {num}** 预热平衡阶段药材内部{unit}的时空分布'
               f'（附录 2 常物性，r = {P.R0_CM:g} cm 列为边界条件重构的表面值）')
        (P.FIG_DIR / f'{tag}.md').write_text(_md(hdr, rows, cap), encoding='utf-8')
        made.append(tag)

    # ---- 表 3、表 4：问题二 3 h 内每 0.5 h x 5 位置
    for tag, key, unit, num in (('TABLE_3', 'table_3_temperature', '温度 / °C', 3),
                                ('TABLE_4', 'table_4_moisture', '含水率 / (kg/kg)', 4)):
        t = r2[key]
        hdr = ['时间 / h'] + [f'r = {r:g} cm' for r in t['radii_cm']]
        rows = [[f"{ts / P.SEC_PER_HOUR:.1f}"] + [_f(v) for v in vals]
                for ts, vals in zip(t['times_s'], t['values'])]
        cap = (f'**表 {num}** 全过程变物性双向强耦合下药材内部{unit}的时空分布'
               f'（附录 3，空气侧取附件 1 实测驱动）')
        (P.FIG_DIR / f'{tag}.md').write_text(_md(hdr, rows, cap), encoding='utf-8')
        made.append(tag)

    # ---- 表 5：问题三 每 6 h x 每 0.5 cm，末行给烘干结束时间
    t5 = r3['table_5_moisture']
    hdr5 = ['时间 / h'] + [f'r = {r:g} cm' for r in t5['radii_cm']]
    rows5 = [[f"{th:.0f}"] + [_f(v) for v in vals]
             for th, vals in zip(t5['times_h'], t5['values'])]
    te = t5['t_end_row']
    rows5.append([f"**{te['label']} {te['hours']:.4f} h**"]
                 + [f"**{_f(v)}**" for v in te['values']])
    cap5 = ('**表 5** 全域最大含水率判据下的含水率分布与烘干结束时间'
            f"（判据：各处含水率均降至 {P.C_THR:g} kg/kg 以下，"
            f"首次满足于 t = {te['hours']:.4f} h）")
    (P.FIG_DIR / 'TABLE_5.md').write_text(_md(hdr5, rows5, cap5), encoding='utf-8')
    made.append('TABLE_5')

    # ---- 表 6：问题四 含收缩，末列为随收缩而变的药材表面
    t6 = r4['table_6_moisture']
    hdr6 = ['时间 / h', '半径 R(t) / cm'] + [f'r = {r:g} cm' for r in t6['radii_cm']] \
        + [t6['surface_label']]
    rows6 = [[f"{r['t_h']:.0f}", f"{r['R_cm']:.4f}"] + [_f(v) for v in r['C']]
             + [_f(r['C_surface'])] for r in t6['rows']]
    te6 = t6['t_end_row']
    rows6.append([f"**{te6['label']} {te6['hours']:.4f} h**", f"**{te6['R_cm']:.4f}**"]
                 + [BLANK if v is None else f"**{_f(v)}**" for v in te6['C']]
                 + [f"**{_f(te6['C_surface'])}**"])
    cap6 = ('**表 6** 径向收缩条件下药材含水率分布与烘干结束时间'
            f"（半径由 {P.R0_CM:g} cm 收缩至 {te6['R_cm']:.4f} cm；"
            f'“{BLANK}” 表示该固定距离已超出当时半径，物理上不存在该点）')
    (P.FIG_DIR / 'TABLE_6.md').write_text(_md(hdr6, rows6, cap6), encoding='utf-8')
    made.append('TABLE_6')

    # ---- 表 7：四级模型阶梯（MC8）；表 8：判据口径对照；表 9：敏感性排序
    ml = r2['model_ladder']['paths']
    name_map = {'L0_lumped': 'L0 集中参数（Bi=O(1)，先验失效）',
                'L1_const_prop': 'L1 常物性（冻结在初值）',
                'L2_oneway': 'L2 单向耦合（切断 T→C 反馈）',
                'L3_full': 'L3 全耦合（本文基准）'}
    base = ml['L3_full']['volavg_C_end']
    rows7 = []
    for k in ('L0_lumped', 'L1_const_prop', 'L2_oneway', 'L3_full'):
        v = ml[k].get('volavg_C_end', ml[k].get('C_end'))
        rows7.append([name_map[k], _f(v), f'{100.0 * (v - base) / base:+.2f}'])
    cap7 = ('**表 7** 四级模型阶梯在 t = 3 h 的体积平均含水率对比'
            '（每级均为独立真实求解，非近似换算）')
    (P.FIG_DIR / 'TABLE_7.md').write_text(
        _md(['模型层级', '体积平均含水率 / (kg/kg)', '相对基准偏差 / %'], rows7, cap7),
        encoding='utf-8')
    made.append('TABLE_7')

    cc = r3['criterion_comparison']
    c17 = r3['checks']['C17']
    lab = {'surface': '表面含水率下穿（最松）', 'volavg': '体积平均含水率下穿（误读“各处”）',
           'max': '全域最大含水率下穿（题面判据）'}
    rows8 = [[lab[k], f"{cc[k]['t_end_h']:.4f}", f"{cc[k]['t_end_h'] / 24:.4f}",
              f"{100.0 * (cc[k]['t_end_h'] - cc['max']['t_end_h']) / cc['max']['t_end_h']:+.2f}"]
             for k in ('surface', 'volavg', 'max')]
    cap8 = ('**表 8** 三种终止判据口径对照'
            f"（误读为体积平均将低估工期 {c17['underestimate_pct']:.2f}%，"
            f"即 {c17['underestimate_by_volavg_h']:.4f} h）")
    (P.FIG_DIR / 'TABLE_8.md').write_text(
        _md(['终止判据', '烘干时长 / h', '烘干时长 / d', '相对题面判据偏差 / %'], rows8, cap8),
        encoding='utf-8')
    made.append('TABLE_8')

    rs = load('sensitivity_results')
    tor, spear = rs['tornado'], rs['lhs']['spearman']
    rows9 = []
    for k in tor['order']:
        b = tor['by_factor'][k]
        amp = (f"±{b['amplitude']:g} °C" if b['kind'] == 'abs'
               else f"±{100 * b['amplitude']:g}%")
        rows9.append([b['label'], amp, f"{b['t_end_h_low']:.4f}", f"{b['t_end_h_high']:.4f}",
                      f"{b['half_range_h']:.4f}", f"{b['elasticity']:+.4f}",
                      f"{spear[k]:+.4f}"])
    cap9 = ('**表 9** 烘干时长的单因子敏感性排序与全局秩相关'
            f"（基准 {tor['baseline_h']:.4f} h；弹性符号全部与机理一致；"
            f"LHS n = {rs['lhs']['n']} 的秩相关首因子与单因子排序首位一致）")
    (P.FIG_DIR / 'TABLE_9.md').write_text(
        _md(['因子', '扰动幅度', '下界 t_end / h', '上界 t_end / h', '半极差 / h',
             '弹性', 'Spearman'], rows9, cap9), encoding='utf-8')
    made.append('TABLE_9')

    # ---- 表 10：问题四 2x2 归因分解
    at = r4['attribution']
    c21 = r4['checks']['C21']
    amap = {'q3_baseline': '附录 3 物性 + 固定域（问题三基准）',
            'app4_noshrink': '附录 4 物性 + 固定域',
            'app3_shrink': '附录 3 物性 + 径向收缩',
            'app4_shrink': '附录 4 物性 + 径向收缩（问题四）'}
    b0 = at['q3_baseline']['t_end_h']
    rows10 = [[amap[k], f"{at[k]['t_end_h']:.4f}", f"{at[k]['t_end_h'] / 24:.4f}",
               f"{100.0 * (at[k]['t_end_h'] - b0) / b0:+.2f}"]
              for k in ('q3_baseline', 'app4_noshrink', 'app3_shrink', 'app4_shrink')]
    # 收缩效应在两个物性基准上各有一个值，必须同时点名，否则读者只能推出交互项为零
    shrink_on_app3 = at['app3_shrink']['t_end_s'] - at['q3_baseline']['t_end_s']
    shrink_on_app4 = at['app4_shrink']['t_end_s'] - at['app4_noshrink']['t_end_s']
    cap10 = ('**表 10** 物性与收缩的 2×2 归因分解（四次独立真实求解）。收缩效应依基准而异：'
             f"附录 3 物性下为 {shrink_on_app3:+.0f} s、附录 4 物性下为 {shrink_on_app4:+.0f} s，"
             f"二者之差 {c21['interaction_s']:+.0f} s 即交互项，占总效应 "
             f"{c21['interaction_over_total_pct']:.2f}%，故加性框架在此失效；"
             f"改用乘性框架，残差（以预测比值 {c21['mult_predicted_ratio']:.4f} 为分母）仅 "
             f"{c21['mult_residual_pct']:.2f}%，说明两种机理近似为乘性叠加")
    (P.FIG_DIR / 'TABLE_10.md').write_text(
        _md(['算例', '烘干时长 / h', '烘干时长 / d', '相对问题三偏差 / %'], rows10, cap10),
        encoding='utf-8')
    made.append('TABLE_10')

    print('生成 %d 个表格文件：%s' % (len(made), ', '.join(f'{m}.md' for m in made)))
    for m in made:
        p = P.FIG_DIR / f'{m}.md'
        n_row = sum(1 for ln in p.read_text(encoding='utf-8').splitlines()
                    if ln.startswith('|')) - 2
        print(f'  figures/{m}.md  {p.stat().st_size:>5d} bytes  {n_row} 数据行')
    return made


if __name__ == '__main__':
    run()
```

**B.12　main.py**

```python
# code/main.py — 总编排：第 0 层读盘核验 -> 四问求解 -> 敏感性 -> 汇总台账与交付清单
import json
import time

import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))   # 本机为嵌入式发行版，不自动加脚本目录

import params as P

STAGES = ('data_check', 'problem1', 'problem2', 'problem3', 'problem4', 'sensitivity')


def _dump(path, obj):
    path.write_text(json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding='utf-8')


def _fmt(k, v):
    return f"{k}: {'PASS' if v.get('passed') else 'FAIL'}  {v.get('name', '')}"


# ------------------------------------------------------------ 交付台账（§12.2）
def cross_problem_ledger(r1, r2, r3, r4):
    """跨问题一致性台账：bounds 为建模阶段写定的先验，observed 由本次真实运行回填。"""
    s1, s2, s3, s4 = r1['summary'], r2['summary'], r3['summary'], r4['summary']
    # XP4 的包络值与出现时刻一律由附件 1 现读，避免 reason 里的溯源数字与数据脱钩
    import utils as U
    ta_peak = float(np.max(U._TA_OBS))
    ta_peak_t = float(U._T_GRID[int(np.argmax(U._TA_OBS))])
    assert abs(ta_peak - 50.2460) < 5e-5, '附件 1 温度包络与建模报告写定的 50.2460 °C 不符'
    items = [
        {'id': 'XP1', 'quantity': '问题一药材温度上界', 'unit': '°C',
         'bound': {'op': '<=', 'value': 41.5130},
         'observed': s1['herb_T_max'],
         'reason': '1800 s 内热风由 28 °C 线性升至 41.5130 °C，药材温度不得超过同期热风温度'},
        {'id': 'XP2', 'quantity': '问题一药材温度下界', 'unit': '°C',
         'bound': {'op': '>=', 'value': P.T0_C},
         'observed': s1['herb_T_min'],
         'reason': '热风全程高于初温 28 °C，故药材只被加热'},
        {'id': 'XP3', 'quantity': '问题一含水率上界', 'unit': 'kg/kg',
         'bound': {'op': '<=', 'value': P.C0},
         'observed': s1['herb_C_max'],
         'reason': '干燥过程只失水，任意点含水率不超过初值 C0'},
        {'id': 'XP4', 'quantity': '问题二药材温度上界', 'unit': '°C',
         'bound': {'op': '<=', 'value': 50.2460},
         'observed': s2['herb_T_max'],
         'reason': '附件 1 热风温度峰值 %.4f °C（现读自附件 1，出现在 t=%.0f s），'
                   '药材温度不得越过驱动包络' % (ta_peak, ta_peak_t)},
        {'id': 'XP5', 'quantity': '问题三与问题二在 t<=10800 s 的场差', 'unit': '°C 或 kg/kg',
         'bound': {'op': '<=', 'value': 1e-9},
         'observed': r3['checks']['XA2']['measured_max_abs_diff'],
         'reason': '同一右端、同一积分设定，问题三前 10800 s 必须与问题二逐格恒等'},
        {'id': 'XP6', 'quantity': '问题三事件时刻全域最大含水率', 'unit': 'kg/kg',
         'bound': {'op': '<=', 'value': P.C_THR + 1e-5},
         'observed': s3['C_max_at_t_end'],
         'reason': '判据即全域最大含水率首次下穿 0.15，事件时刻必须贴住阈值'},
        {'id': 'XP7', 'quantity': '问题四半径下界', 'unit': 'cm',
         'bound': {'op': '>=', 'value': 1.198},
         'observed': s4['R_min_cm'],
         'reason': '附件 2 末点半径 1.198 cm，保单调插值不得越出实测下界'},
        {'id': 'XP8', 'quantity': '问题四半径上界', 'unit': 'cm',
         'bound': {'op': '<=', 'value': P.R0_CM},
         'observed': s4['R_max_cm'],
         'reason': '半径单调不增，起点即为最大值 2.0 cm'},
        {'id': 'XP9', 'quantity': '问题三、四烘干时长量级', 'unit': 'd',
         'bound': {'op': 'in', 'value': [0.1 * P.DUR_DAYS_LOW, 10 * P.DUR_DAYS_HIGH]},
         'observed': [s3['t_end_days'], s4['t_end_days']],
         'reason': '题面「一般持续 2-3 天」是独立量级参照，允许同量级偏离，不作标定目标'},
    ]
    viol = []
    for it in items:
        op, val, obs = it['bound']['op'], it['bound']['value'], it['observed']
        obs_list = obs if isinstance(obs, list) else [obs]
        if op == '<=':
            ok = all(float(o) <= val + 1e-12 for o in obs_list)
        elif op == '>=':
            ok = all(float(o) >= val - 1e-12 for o in obs_list)
        else:
            ok = all(val[0] <= float(o) <= val[1] for o in obs_list)
        it['satisfied'] = bool(ok)
        if not ok:
            viol.append(it['id'])
    return {'n_items': len(items), 'items': items, 'violations': viol,
            'all_satisfied': not viol,
            'cross_problem_check_module': '_utils/cross_problem_check.py',
            'module_available': (P.ROOT / '_utils' / 'cross_problem_check.py').exists(),
            'note': 'SKILL 第 7 步引用的 _utils/cross_problem_check.py 在本运行时不存在；'
                    '按「缺少内部模块时停止并报告模块名」的要求，此处只如实记录模块名，'
                    '台账本身按 §12.2 的 bound/observed/reason 三段式由真实运行回填'}


def run_all():
    t0 = time.time()
    import data_check
    import problem1 as Q1
    import problem2 as Q2
    import problem3 as Q3
    import problem4 as Q4
    import sensitivity_analysis as S

    timing, checks = {}, {}

    tk = time.time()
    v0, v0_fail = data_check.run()
    timing['data_check'] = time.time() - tk
    assert not v0_fail, '第 0 层读盘核验失败：%s' % ','.join(v0_fail)

    print('\n===== 问题一 =====')
    tk = time.time()
    r1 = Q1.run()[0]
    timing['problem1'] = time.time() - tk
    for k, v in r1['checks'].items():
        print(_fmt(k, v))

    print('\n===== 问题二 =====')
    tk = time.time()
    r2 = Q2.run()[0]
    timing['problem2'] = time.time() - tk
    for k, v in r2['checks'].items():
        print(_fmt(k, v))

    print('\n===== 问题三 =====')
    tk = time.time()
    r3 = Q3.run()[0]
    timing['problem3'] = time.time() - tk
    for k, v in r3['checks'].items():
        print(_fmt(k, v))

    print('\n===== 问题四 =====')
    tk = time.time()
    r4 = Q4.run()[0]
    timing['problem4'] = time.time() - tk
    for k, v in r4['checks'].items():
        print(_fmt(k, v))

    print('\n===== 敏感性与不确定度 =====')
    tk = time.time()
    rs = S.run()[0]
    timing['sensitivity'] = time.time() - tk
    for k, v in rs['checks'].items():
        print(_fmt(k, v))

    P.FIG_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    for name, obj in (('problem_1_results', r1), ('problem_2_results', r2),
                      ('problem_3_results', r3), ('problem_4_results', r4),
                      ('sensitivity_results', rs)):
        _dump(P.FIG_DIR / f'{name}.json', obj)

    for tag, r in (('problem1', r1), ('problem2', r2), ('problem3', r3),
                   ('problem4', r4), ('sensitivity', rs)):
        checks[tag] = r['checks']

    ledger = cross_problem_ledger(r1, r2, r3, r4)
    _dump(P.ROOT / 'CROSS_PROBLEM_LEDGER.json', ledger)

    print('\n===== 论文表格（供写稿步骤直接嵌入）=====')
    import make_tables
    make_tables.run()

    n_ck = sum(len(v) for v in checks.values())
    n_pass = sum(1 for v in checks.values() for c in v.values() if c.get('passed'))
    all_ok = bool(n_ck == n_pass and ledger['all_satisfied'])

    allres = {
        'competition': 'cumcm', 'problem_id': 'A', 'title': '药材的烘干问题',
        'generated_by': 'code/main.py', 'seed': P.SEED,
        'n_checks': n_ck, 'n_checks_passed': n_pass, 'all_checks_passed': all_ok,
        'timing_s': timing, 'wall_time_s': time.time() - t0,
        'headline': {
            'q1_T_center_1800s_C': r1['summary']['T_center_1800s'],
            'q1_T_surface_1800s_C': r1['summary']['T_surface_1800s'],
            'q1_C_center_1800s': r1['summary']['C_center_1800s'],
            'q1_C_surface_1800s': r1['summary']['C_surface_1800s'],
            'q1_semi_analytic_max_dev_C': r1['checks']['C09']['measured_max_abs_C'],
            'q1_Bi_T': r1['characteristics']['Bi_T'], 'q1_Bi_m': r1['characteristics']['Bi_m'],
            'q2_T_center_10800s_C': r2['summary']['T_center_end'],
            'q2_T_surface_10800s_C': r2['summary']['T_surface_end'],
            'q2_C_center_10800s': r2['summary']['C_center_end'],
            'q2_C_surface_10800s': r2['summary']['C_surface_end'],
            'q2_volavg_C_10800s': r2['summary']['volavg_C_end'],
            'q3_t_end_h': r3['summary']['t_end_h'], 'q3_t_end_d': r3['summary']['t_end_days'],
            'q4_t_end_h': r4['summary']['t_end_h'], 'q4_t_end_d': r4['summary']['t_end_days'],
            'q4_R_at_t_end_cm': r4['summary']['R_at_t_end_cm'],
            'q4_water_removed_pct': r4['summary']['water_removed_pct'],
            'sens_baseline_t_end_h': rs['baseline']['t_end_h'],
            'sens_top_factor': rs['tornado']['order'][0],
            'sens_lhs_p05_p50_p95_h': [rs['lhs']['t_end_h']['p05'], rs['lhs']['t_end_h']['p50'],
                                       rs['lhs']['t_end_h']['p95']],
        },
        'model_ladder': r2['model_ladder'],          # MC8 阶梯同时暴露在 Q2 与总汇
        'criterion_comparison': r3['criterion_comparison'],
        'q4_attribution': r4['attribution'],
        'cross_problem_ledger': ledger,
        'checks': checks,
        'result_files': {str(k): P.RESULT_FILES[k].name for k in (1, 2, 3, 4)},
    }
    _dump(P.FIG_DIR / 'all_results.json', allres)

    items = []
    for m, desc in (('params', '参数唯一来源：由 PROBLEM_FACTS.json 派生，禁止手改数值'),
                    ('utils', '共用核心：守恒型有限体积、Robin 表面重构、驱动插值、材料坐标右端、写盘'),
                    ('data_check', '第 0 层读盘与口径核验（V0.1—V0.5）'),
                    ('problem1', '问题一：预热平衡阶段单向解耦场 + 贝塞尔-杜阿梅尔半解析对照'),
                    ('problem2', '问题二：全过程变物性双向强耦合 + 四级模型阶梯'),
                    ('problem3', '问题三：全域最大值判据下的烘干时长（事件检测）'),
                    ('problem4', '问题四：径向收缩移动边界（材料坐标）+ 2×2 归因分解'),
                    ('sensitivity_analysis', '第 3 层：单因子 OAT、7×7 响应面、LHS 全局不确定度'),
                    ('sanity_check', '结果自检汇总：只打印结论与至多 5 条违规样本'),
                    ('constraint_audit', '约束复核：C01—C23 与 V 系列判据在写盘 JSON 上重新断言'),
                    ('make_tables', '由 JSON 生成 figures/TABLE_*.md，供写稿步骤直接嵌入'),
                    ('main', '总编排入口：python code/main.py')):
        items.append({'path': f'code/{m}.py', 'desc': desc})
    items.append({'path': 'code/requirements.txt', 'desc': '运行环境版本锁定'})
    for n, desc in (('problem_1_results', '问题一全部数值结果与自检'),
                    ('problem_2_results', '问题二全部数值结果、四级模型阶梯与 D 响应面'),
                    ('problem_3_results', '问题三烘干时长、三判据对照与网格阶梯'),
                    ('problem_4_results', '问题四收缩解、守恒试验与归因分解'),
                    ('sensitivity_results', '敏感性与不确定度（268 次独立真实重算）'),
                    ('all_results', '四问 + 敏感性的总汇与跨问题台账')):
        items.append({'path': f'figures/{n}.json', 'desc': desc})
    for k in range(1, 11):
        items.append({'path': f'figures/TABLE_{k}.md', 'desc': f'论文表 {k}（Markdown，数据取自 JSON）'})
    for k in (1, 2, 3, 4):
        items.append({'path': f'result{k}.xlsx', 'desc': f'竞赛结果文件 result{k}.xlsx（按模板写盘并读回核验）'})
    for p, desc in (('RESULTS.md', '本步骤结果报告'),
                    ('CROSS_PROBLEM_LEDGER.json', '跨问题一致性台账（observed 由真实运行回填）'),
                    ('CAPABILITY_VERDICT.json', '能力项结论与定位证据'),
                    ('figures/constraint_audit.json', '约束复核逐条总账')):
        items.append({'path': p, 'desc': desc})

    deliv = {
        'deliverables': items,
        'step': 'comp-code', 'competition': 'cumcm', 'problem_id': 'A', 'language': 'zh',
        'n_deliverables': len(items),
        'n_checks': n_ck, 'n_checks_passed': n_pass, 'all_checks_passed': all_ok,
        'timing_s': timing, 'wall_time_s': time.time() - t0,
        'entry_point': 'python code/main.py',
        'fast_mode': False, 'sampling': False, 'full_dataset': True,
        'sampling_note': '全量计算，无抽样：附件 1 的 241 点与附件 2 的 145 点全部参与；'
                         'result1/2 逐秒输出（1800 与 10800 行），result3/4 逐 60 s 输出至 t_end；'
                         '敏感性的 268 个算例每个都独立积分到事件触发，无插值外推',
    }
    _dump(P.ROOT / 'DELIVERABLES.json', deliv)

    print('\n===== 汇总 =====')
    print('checks %d/%d passed | ledger %s | wall %.1f s'
          % (n_pass, n_ck, 'OK' if ledger['all_satisfied'] else 'VIOLATION',
             time.time() - t0))
    for k, v in timing.items():
        print('  %-12s %7.1f s' % (k, v))
    print('ALL PASSED:', all_ok)
    return allres


if __name__ == '__main__':
    out = run_all()
    raise SystemExit(0 if out['all_checks_passed'] else 1)
```
